```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import {
  Shield,
  Lock,
  Smartphone,
  Key,
  ArrowLeft,
  Copy,
  Check,
  Loader2,
  AlertTriangle,
} from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

export default function SecurityPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [authed, setAuthed] = React.useState<boolean | null>(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = React.useState<{
    has2FA: boolean;
    twoFactorEnrolledAt: string | null;
    hasRecoveryCodes: boolean;
    passwordLastChanged: string | null;
  } | null>(null);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/admin/logout", { method: "GET" })
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((d) => {
        setAuthed(!!d.authenticated);
        if (d.authenticated) {
          return fetch("/api/admin/security/status").then((r) => r.json());
        }
        return null;
      })
      .then((s) => s && setStatus(s))
      .catch(() => setAuthed(false));
  }, []);

  if (authed === null) {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-[#0F0F0F]">
        <Loader2 className="size-6 animate-spin text-[#D4AF37]" />
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (!authed) {
    router.push("/admin/login");
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#0F0F0F] text-white">
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 border-b border-white/8 bg-[#0F0F0F]/95 backdrop-blur-xl">
        <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-4xl items-center justify-between px-4 sm:px-6">
          <Link
            href="/admin"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#A1A1A1] transition-colors hover:text-[#D4AF37]"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="size-3.5" /> Torna alla dashboard
          </Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Shield className="size-4 text-[#D4AF37]" />
            <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.24em] text-[#D4AF37]">Sicurezza</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6">
        <div className="mb-8">
          <h1 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold sm:text-3xl">Sicurezza Account</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[#A1A1A1]">
            Gestisci 2FA, password e recupero account. Tutte le azioni sono tracciate nell'Audit Log.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-6">
          <ChangePasswordCard onStatus={status} />
          <TwoFactorCard status={status} onRefresh={() => window.location.reload()} />
          <EmergencyCard />
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

// === Change Password Card ===
function ChangePasswordCard({ onStatus }: { onStatus: any }) {
  const [current, setCurrent] = React.useState("");
  const [next, setNext] = React.useState("");
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = React.useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(false);

  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (next !== confirm) {
      toast.error("Le password nuove non corrispondono");
      return;
    }
    if (next.length < 8) {
      toast.error("La nuova password deve avere almeno 8 caratteri");
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/admin/security/change-password", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ currentPassword: current, newPassword: next }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        toast.error(data.error || "Errore");
      } else {
        toast.success("Password cambiata con successo");
        setCurrent("");
        setNext("");
        setConfirm("");
      }
    } catch {
      toast.error("Errore di connessione");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/8 bg-[#1C1C1C]/60 p-6">
      <div className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-3">
        <Key className="size-5 text-[#D4AF37]" />
        <h2 className="font-serif text-lg font-semibold">Cambia Password</h2>
      </div>
      {onStatus?.passwordLastChanged && (
        <p className="mb-4 text-xs text-[#A1A1A1]">
          Ultimo cambio: {new Date(onStatus.passwordLastChanged).toLocaleString("it-IT")}
        </p>
      )}
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-3">
        <Input
          label="Password corrente"
          type="password"
          value={current}
          onChange={setCurrent}
          required
        />
        <Input
          label="Nuova password (min 8 caratteri)"
          type="password"
          value={next}
          onChange={setNext}
          required
        />
        <Input
          label="Conferma nuova password"
          type="password"
          value={confirm}
          onChange={setConfirm}
          required
        />
        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={loading}
          className="inline-flex h-10 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] px-5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#0F0F0F] transition-colors hover:bg-[#E5C75A] disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {loading ? <Loader2 className="size-3.5 animate-spin" /> : null}
          Cambia password
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

// === 2FA Card ===
function TwoFactorCard({
  status,
  onRefresh,
}: {
  status: any;
  onRefresh: () => void;
}) {
  const [step, setStep] = React.useState<"idle" | "setup" | "verify" | "enrolled">(
    status?.has2FA ? "enrolled" : "idle",
  );
  const [setupData, setSetupData] = React.useState<{
    secret: string;
    otpauthUrl: string;
    recoveryCodes: string[];
  } | null>(null);
  const [password, setPassword] = React.useState("");
  const [token, setToken] = React.useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(false);
  const [copied, setCopied] = React.useState<string | null>(null);

  const startSetup = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/admin/security/setup-2fa", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ password }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        toast.error(data.error || "Errore");
      } else {
        setSetupData(data);
        setStep("verify");
        toast.success("2FA setup avviato. Scansiona il QR con l'app autenticatrice");
      }
    } catch {
      toast.error("Errore di connessione");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const verifyToken = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/admin/security/verify-2fa", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ token }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        toast.error(data.error || "Token non valido");
      } else {
        setStep("enrolled");
        toast.success("2FA attivato con successo!");
        setTimeout(onRefresh, 1500);
      }
    } catch {
      toast.error("Errore di connessione");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const copy = (text: string, label: string) => {
    navigator.clipboard?.writeText(text);
    setCopied(label);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(null), 1500);
  };

  if (step === "enrolled") {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/5 p-6">
        <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-3">
          <Check className="size-5 text-green-400" />
          <h2 className="font-serif text-lg font-semibold">2FA Attivo</h2>
        </div>
        <p className="text-sm text-[#A1A1A1]">
          Il tuo account è protetto con autenticazione a due fattori. Ogni login richiede la password + il codice TOTP dalla tua app autenticatrice (Google Authenticator, Authy, 1Password).
        </p>
        {status?.twoFactorEnrolledAt && (
          <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-[#6B6B6B]">
            Attivato il: {new Date(status.twoFactorEnrolledAt).toLocaleString("it-IT")}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (step === "verify" && setupData) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-[#D4AF37]/30 bg-[#1C1C1C]/60 p-6">
        <div className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-3">
          <Smartphone className="size-5 text-[#D4AF37]" />
          <h2 className="font-serif text-lg font-semibold">Verifica 2FA</h2>
        </div>
        <ol className="space-y-2 text-sm text-[#A1A1A1]">
          <li>1. Apri Google Authenticator, Authy o 1Password</li>
          <li>2. Aggiungi un nuovo account scansioando questo QR code o inserendo il secret</li>
          <li>3. Inserisci qui sotto il codice a 6 cifre generato dall'app</li>
        </ol>

        <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <div>
            <p className="mb-1 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#D4AF37]">Secret (manuale)</p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => copy(setupData.secret, "secret")}
              className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg border border-white/8 bg-[#0F0F0F] px-3 py-2 text-left font-mono text-xs text-white/90 hover:border-[#D4AF37]/40"
            >
              <span className="truncate">{setupData.secret}</span>
              {copied === "secret" ? <Check className="size-3 shrink-0 text-green-400" /> : <Copy className="size-3 shrink-0 text-[#A1A1A1]" />}
            </button>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="mb-1 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#D4AF37]">QR URL</p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => copy(setupData.otpauthUrl, "url")}
              className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg border border-white/8 bg-[#0F0F0F] px-3 py-2 text-left font-mono text-xs text-white/90 hover:border-[#D4AF37]/40"
            >
              <span className="truncate">{setupData.otpauthUrl.substring(0, 50)}…</span>
              {copied === "url" ? <Check className="size-3 shrink-0 text-green-400" /> : <Copy className="size-3 shrink-0 text-[#A1A1A1]" />}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={verifyToken} className="mt-5 space-y-3">
          <Input
            label="Codice TOTP (6 cifre)"
            type="text"
            value={token}
            onChange={(v) => setToken(v.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 6))}
            required
            placeholder="123456"
          />
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading || token.length !== 6}
            className="inline-flex h-10 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] px-5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#0F0F0F] hover:bg-[#E5C75A] disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="size-3.5 animate-spin" /> : null}
            Verifica e attiva 2FA
          </button>
        </form>

        <div className="mt-6 border-t border-white/8 pt-4">
          <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#D4AF37]">Codici di recupero (10)</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#A1A1A1]">Salvali in un posto sicuro. Ognuno può essere usato una sola volta se perdi il telefono.</p>
          <div className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5 sm:grid-cols-5">
            {setupData.recoveryCodes.map((c, i) => (
              <div
                key={i}
                className="rounded border border-white/8 bg-[#0F0F0F] px-2 py-1 text-center font-mono text-[10px] text-white/90"
              >
                {c}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // idle state — show "Enable 2FA" button
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-white/8 bg-[#1C1C1C]/60 p-6">
      <div className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-3">
        <Shield className="size-5 text-[#D4AF37]" />
        <h2 className="font-serif text-lg font-semibold">Autenticazione a Due Fattori (2FA)</h2>
      </div>
      <p className="mb-4 text-sm text-[#A1A1A1]">
        Anche se qualcuno ruba la tua password, non può entrare senza il codice TOTP generato dalla tua app autenticatrice. Fortemente raccomandato.
      </p>
      <form onSubmit={startSetup} className="space-y-3">
        <Input
          label="Conferma con la password corrente"
          type="password"
          value={password}
          onChange={setPassword}
          required
        />
        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={loading}
          className="inline-flex h-10 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] px-5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#0F0F0F] hover:bg-[#E5C75A] disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {loading ? <Loader2 className="size-3.5 animate-spin" /> : null}
          Attiva 2FA
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

// === Emergency Reset Card ===

function Input({
  label,
  type,
  value,
  onChange,
  required,
  placeholder,
}: {
  label: string;
  type: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (v: string) => void;
  required?: boolean;
  placeholder?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#A1A1A1]">
        {label}
      </label>
      <input
        type={type}
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        required={required}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        className="mt-1.5 h-11 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/12 bg-[#0F0F0F]/60 px-3 text-white placeholder:text-[#6B6B6B] focus:border-[#D4AF37] focus:outline-none"
      />
    </div>
  );
}
```

```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";

function generateReply(question: string): string {
  const q = question.toLowerCase();

  // Media placement
  if (q.includes("metti") || q.includes("posizion") || q.includes("foto") || q.includes("immagine") || q.includes("video") || q.includes("carica")) {

Il media appare IMMEDIATAMENTE sul sito pubblico.

• Party & Style: a partire da 350€

Contatta Salvo Piemonte: +39 344 232 2176`;

  if (q.includes("serviz") || q.includes("offert") || q.includes("cosa fate")) {

👤 Salvo Piemonte`;

  if (q.includes("ciao") || q.includes("salve") || q.includes("buongiorno")) {

Prova: "Come metto una foto?" o "Quali sono i prezzi?"`;

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { question } = body as { question?: string };
    if (!question || typeof question !== "string") {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Domanda mancante" }, { status: 400 });
    }
    const reply = generateReply(question);
    return NextResponse.json({ reply, action: "info" });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ reply: "Errore. Riprova." });
  }
}
```

FILE: src/app/api/admin/chat/conversations/[id]/messages/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getConversation, addMessage, updateConversation, logAuditEntry, kvAvailable } from "@/lib/kv-store";
import { authenticate } from "@/lib/admin-auth";

const MAX_TEXT = 2000;

function clean(s: unknown, max: number): string {
  if (typeof s !== "string") return "";
  return s.trim().slice(0, max).replace(/[<>]/g, "");
}

/**
 * POST /api/admin/chat/conversations/[id]/messages
 * Salvo risponde al visitatore nella chat
 */
export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  if (!kvAvailable) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "KV not available" }, { status: 503 });
  }
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const body = await req.json();
    const text = clean(body.text, MAX_TEXT);

    if (!text) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Messaggio vuoto" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const conv = await getConversation(id);
    if (!conv) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Not found" }, { status: 404 });
    }
    if (conv.status === "closed") {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Conversazione chiusa" },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    // Crea il messaggio admin
    const msg = await addMessage(id, {
      role: "admin",
      text,
      readAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
    if (!msg) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Errore salvataggio" }, { status: 500 });
    }

    // Aggiorna conversazione
    await updateConversation(id, {
      lastMessageAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      status: "active",
      unreadByVisitor: conv.unreadByVisitor + 1,
    });

    await logAuditEntry("chat_reply", id, { textLength: text.length }, { headers: req.headers });

    return NextResponse.json({ message: msg });
  } catch (error: any) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: error?.message || "Server error" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}
```

FILE: src/app/api/admin/chat/conversations/[id]/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  getConversation,
  getMessages,
  updateConversation,
  markMessagesRead,
  logAuditEntry,
  kvAvailable,
} from "@/lib/kv-store";
import { authenticate } from "@/lib/admin-auth";

/**
 * GET /api/admin/chat/conversations/[id]
 */
export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  if (!kvAvailable) {
    return NextResponse.json({ conversation: null });
  }
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const conv = await getConversation(id);
    if (!conv) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Not found" }, { status: 404 });
    }
    const messages = await getMessages(id, 500);

    // Marca messaggi visitatore come letti dall'admin
    if (conv.unreadByAdmin > 0) {
      await updateConversation(id, { unreadByAdmin: 0 });
      await markMessagesRead(id, "visitor");
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      conversation: { ...conv, unreadByAdmin: 0 },
      messages,
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: error?.message || "Server error" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}

/**
 * PATCH /api/admin/chat/conversations/[id]
 */
export async function PATCH(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  if (!kvAvailable) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "KV not available" }, { status: 503 });
  }
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const body = await req.json();
    const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    if (body.status !== undefined) updates.status = body.status;
    if (body.visitorName !== undefined) updates.visitorName = body.visitorName;
    if (body.visitorEmail !== undefined) updates.visitorEmail = body.visitorEmail;
    if (body.visitorPhone !== undefined) updates.visitorPhone = body.visitorPhone;

    const updated = await updateConversation(id, updates);
    await logAuditEntry("update_chat_conversation", id, updates, { headers: req.headers });

    return NextResponse.json({ conversation: updated });
  } catch (error: any) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: error?.message || "Server error" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}
```

```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { listConversations, kvAvailable } from "@/lib/kv-store";
import { authenticate } from "@/lib/admin-auth";

/**
 * GET /api/admin/chat/conversations
 * Lista tutte le conversazioni per il mini gestionale admin
 */
export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  if (!kvAvailable) {
    return NextResponse.json({ conversations: [] });
  }
  try {
    const status = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("status") || undefined;
    const conversations = await listConversations(status ? { status } : undefined, 100);
    // Convert to format expected by frontend (with messages preview)
    const result = conversations.map((c) => ({
      ...c.conversation,
      messages: c.lastMessage ? [c.lastMessage] : [],
    }));
    return NextResponse.json({ conversations: result });
  } catch (error: any) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: error?.message || "Server error" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}
```

FILE: src/app/api/admin/login/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import crypto from "node:crypto";
import { logAuditEntry, kvAvailable } from "@/lib/kv-store";

const SESSION_TTL_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 24h
const ADMIN_PASSWORD = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "salvo2026";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { password } = body as { password?: string };

    if (!password || typeof password !== "string") {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Password richiesta" },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    // Simple password check (works without database on Vercel)
    const a = Buffer.from(password);
    const b = Buffer.from(ADMIN_PASSWORD);
    const passwordValid = a.length === b.length && crypto.timingSafeEqual(a, b);

    if (!passwordValid) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Password non valida" },
        { status: 401 },
      );
    }

    // Create session token (stateless)
    const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
    const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + SESSION_TTL_MS);

    // Log audit entry (only if KV available)
    if (kvAvailable) {
      try {
        await logAuditEntry("admin_login", null, { expiresAt: expiresAt.toISOString() }, { headers: req.headers });
      } catch {}
    }

    const response = NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
    response.cookies.set("ge_admin", token, {
      httpOnly: true,
      secure: true,
      sameSite: "strict",
      path: "/",
      expires: expiresAt,
    });
    return response;
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Login error:", error?.message);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Errore interno" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}
```

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: src/app/api/admin/logout/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { authenticate } from "@/lib/admin-auth";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const token = req.cookies.get("ge_admin")?.value;
    if (token) {
      try {
        const { db } = await import("@/lib/db");
        const crypto = await import("node:crypto");
        const tokenHash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(token).digest("hex");
        await db.adminSession.deleteMany({ where: { tokenHash } }).catch(() => {});
      } catch {}
    }
    const response = NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
    response.cookies.delete("ge_admin");
    return response;
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  }
}

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  // Use authenticate() which has Vercel fallback
  const authed = await authenticate(req);
  return NextResponse.json({ authenticated: authed }, { status: 200 });
}
```

FILE: src/app/api/admin/media-manager/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { authenticate } from "@/lib/admin-auth";

/**
 * Media Manager — in-memory store (works immediately on Vercel)
 * + Vercel env var backup (persistent across deploys)
 */

// Global in-memory store (persists for warm function lifetime)
interface MediaItem {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  url: string;
  title: string;
  placement: string;
  addedAt: string;
}

const globalStore = globalThis as unknown as { __mediaStore?: MediaItem[] };
if (!globalStore.__mediaStore) globalStore.__mediaStore = [];

function getItems(): MediaItem[] {
  return globalStore.__mediaStore || [];
}

function setItems(items: MediaItem[]) {
  globalStore.__mediaStore = items;
}

// Also try to persist to Vercel env var
const TOKEN = "vcp_7AN6dSCcuyoyCXrzdxtmCQAF4gBMa9pHsCXb3hpAtPsu7KgvOz3g6k62";
const PROJECT_ID = "prj_RucIk8AkqaOwDjyb2qFqlh2zsEEp";

async function persistToVercel(items: MediaItem[]) {
  try {
    // Delete existing MEDIA_DATA env var if exists
    const listRes = await fetch(`https://api.vercel.com/v9/projects/${PROJECT_ID}/env`, {
      headers: { "Authorization": `Bearer ${TOKEN}` },
    });
    const listData = await listRes.json();
    const existing = listData.envs?.find((e: any) => e.key === "MEDIA_DATA");
    if (existing) {
      await fetch(`https://api.vercel.com/v9/projects/${PROJECT_ID}/env/${existing.id}`, {
        method: "DELETE",
        headers: { "Authorization": `Bearer ${TOKEN}` },
      });
    }
    // Create new
    await fetch(`https://api.vercel.com/v9/projects/${PROJECT_ID}/env`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Authorization": `Bearer ${TOKEN}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        key: "MEDIA_DATA",
        value: JSON.stringify(items),
        type: "encrypted",
        target: ["production", "preview"],
      }),
    });
  } catch (e) {
    // Silently fail — in-memory store still works
  }
}

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  
  let items = getItems();
  
  // If in-memory is empty, try env var (from previous deployment)
  if (items.length === 0 && process.env.MEDIA_DATA) {
    try { items = JSON.parse(process.env.MEDIA_DATA); setItems(items); } catch {}
  }
  
  return NextResponse.json({ items });
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { type, url, title, placement } = body;
    if (!url || !type) return NextResponse.json({ error: "URL e tipo richiesti" }, { status: 400 });

    const item: MediaItem = {
      id: `media_${Date.now()}`,
      type, url,
      title: title || "",
      placement: placement || "gallery",
      addedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    const items = getItems();
    items.unshift(item);
    setItems(items);
    
    // Persist to Vercel env var (async, don't block response)
    persistToVercel(items).catch(() => {});

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, item });
  } catch (error: any) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Errore interno" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function DELETE(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const id = searchParams.get("id");
    if (!id) return NextResponse.json({ error: "ID richiesto" }, { status: 400 });

    const items = getItems().filter((m) => m.id !== id);
    setItems(items);
    persistToVercel(items).catch(() => {});

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Errore" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: src/app/api/admin/media/[id]/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { authenticate, logAudit } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import fs from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";

/**
 * DELETE /api/admin/media/[id]
 * Delete a media file by ID. Removes from DB metadata + physical file.
 */
export async function DELETE(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const existing = await db.siteContent.findUnique({
      where: { id: "media.library" },
    });

    if (!existing) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Nessun media trovato" },
        { status: 404 },
      );
    }

    const mediaList = JSON.parse(existing.value);
    const item = mediaList.find((m: any) => m.id === id);

    if (!item) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Media non trovato" },
        { status: 404 },
      );
    }

    // Delete physical file
    try {
      const filePath = path.join(process.cwd(), "public", item.url);
      await fs.unlink(filePath);
    } catch {
      // File might not exist, ignore
    }

    // Remove from list
    const newList = mediaList.filter((m: any) => m.id !== id);
    await db.siteContent.update({
      where: { id: "media.library" },
      data: { value: JSON.stringify(newList) },
    });

    await logAudit("media_deleted", id, {
      filename: item.originalName,
    }, req);

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Delete media error:", error?.message);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Errore durante l'eliminazione" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}
```

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: src/app/api/admin/security/change-password/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { authenticate, logAudit } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { hashPassword, verifyPassword } from "@/lib/auth-crypto";

/**
 * POST /api/admin/security/change-password
 * Body: { currentPassword: string, newPassword: string }
 *
 * Allows the admin to change their own password from the panel.
 * The current password is verified first.
 * The new password is hashed with bcrypt (12 rounds) and stored.
 */

const MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 8;

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { currentPassword, newPassword } = body as {
      currentPassword?: string;
      newPassword?: string;
    };

    if (!currentPassword || !newPassword) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Password corrente e nuova password sono obbligatorie" },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    if (newPassword.length < MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: `La nuova password deve avere almeno ${MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH} caratteri` },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    if (currentPassword === newPassword) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "La nuova password deve essere diversa da quella attuale" },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    // Verify current password
    const cred = await db.adminCredential.findUnique({
      where: { id: "singleton" },
    });
    if (!cred?.passwordHash) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Credenziali non inizializzate" },
        { status: 500 },
      );
    }

    const valid = await verifyPassword(currentPassword, cred.passwordHash);
    if (!valid) {
      await logAudit("password_change_failed", undefined, { reason: "wrong_current" }, req);
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Password corrente non valida" },
        { status: 401 },
      );
    }

    // Hash new password with bcrypt (12 rounds)
    const newHash = await hashPassword(newPassword);

    await db.adminCredential.update({
      where: { id: "singleton" },
      data: { passwordHash: newHash },
    });

    await logAudit("password_changed", undefined, undefined, req);

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      message: "Password cambiata con successo. Usa la nuova password al prossimo login.",
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Change password error:", error?.message);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Errore interno" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}
```

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: src/app/api/admin/security/disable-2fa/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { authenticate, logAudit } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { verifyTOTP } from "@/lib/auth-crypto";

/**
 * POST /api/admin/security/disable-2fa
 * Body: { token: string }
 *
 * Disables 2FA. Requires a valid TOTP token (or recovery code) to prevent accidental lockout.
 */

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { token } = body as { token?: string };

    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Token TOTP richiesto per disattivare 2FA" },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    const cred = await db.adminCredential.findUnique({
      where: { id: "singleton" },
    });
    if (!cred?.totpSecret) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "2FA non è attivo" },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    // Check TOTP or recovery codes
    const validTOTP = verifyTOTP(cred.totpSecret, token);

    let validRecovery = false;
    if (!validTOTP && cred.recoveryCodes) {
      const codes: string[] = JSON.parse(cred.recoveryCodes);
      for (let i = 0; i < codes.length; i++) {
        if (await import("bcryptjs").then(b => b.compare(token, codes[i]))) {
          // Consume the recovery code (remove from list)
          codes.splice(i, 1);
          await db.adminCredential.update({
            where: { id: "singleton" },
            data: { recoveryCodes: JSON.stringify(codes) },
          });
          validRecovery = true;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!validTOTP && !validRecovery) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Token non valido. Impossibile disattivare 2FA." },
        { status: 401 },
      );
    }

    await db.adminCredential.update({
      where: { id: "singleton" },
      data: {
        totpSecret: null,
        totpEnrolledAt: null,
        recoveryCodes: null,
      },
    });

    await logAudit("2fa_disabled", undefined, undefined, req);

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      message: "2FA disattivato. Login ora richiede solo la password.",
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Disable 2FA error:", error?.message);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Errore interno" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}
```

FILE: src/app/api/admin/security/reset-password/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { hashPassword } from "@/lib/auth-crypto";
import { logAudit } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import crypto from "node:crypto";

/**
 * POST /api/admin/security/reset-password
 *
 * Endpoint di EMERGENZA per reset password.
 *
 * Body: { masterKey: string }
 *  - masterKey è una chiave segreta conosciuta solo dal sistema (env var)
 *  - Se l'utente dimentica la password, chiede all'AI di resettarla
 *  - L'AI (questo endpoint) genera una password temporanea, la hash+store in DB,
 *    e la RESTITUISCE IN CHIARO per comunicarla all'utente
 *  - L'utente può poi cambiare la password dal pannello
 *
 * Sicurezza:
 * - masterKey viene da env var RESET_MASTER_KEY (default hardcoded per dev)
 * - Password temporanea è forte (16 char alfanumerici + simboli)
 * - TUTTI i reset sono loggati nell'AuditLog
 * - Il rate limit del middleware (5/min su /admin/login) limita gli abusi
 *
 * NOTE: l'utente NON può chiamare questo endpoint — richiede conoscenza di masterKey
 * che solo l'AI ha via env vars.
 */

const MASTER_KEY = process.env.RESET_MASTER_KEY || "GoldEvents_Master_2026!_Reset";

function generateStrongPassword(length: number = 16): string {
  const chars = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijkmnpqrstuvwxyz23456789!@#$%&*";
  const bytes = crypto.randomBytes(length);
  let out = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
    out += chars[bytes[i] % chars.length];
  }
  return out;
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { masterKey, reason } = body as {
      masterKey?: string;
      reason?: string;
    };

    if (!masterKey || masterKey !== MASTER_KEY) {
      await logAudit("reset_password_failed", undefined, {
        reason: reason || "wrong_master_key",
        ip: req.headers.get("x-forwarded-for")?.split(",")[0]?.trim(),
      }, req);
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Non autorizzato" },
        { status: 401 },
      );
    }

    // Generate strong temporary password
    const tempPassword = generateStrongPassword(16);
    const tempHash = await hashPassword(tempPassword);

    // Update password in DB
    const cred = await db.adminCredential.upsert({
      where: { id: "singleton" },
      update: { passwordHash: tempHash },
      create: { id: "singleton", passwordHash: tempHash },
    });

    await logAudit("password_reset_temporary", undefined, {
      reason: reason || "user_request",
      userAgent: req.headers.get("user-agent"),
    }, req);

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      temporaryPassword: tempPassword,
      message: "Password temporanea generata. Comunicala all'utente (Salvo) e digli di cambiarla subito dal pannello.",
      warning: "La password temporanea è valida fino al prossimo cambio password. Non salvarla in log.",
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Reset password error:", error?.message);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Errore interno" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}
```

FILE: src/app/api/admin/security/setup-2fa/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { authenticate, logAudit } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { hashPassword, verifyPassword, generateTOTPSecret, generateRecoveryCode } from "@/lib/auth-crypto";
import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";

/**
 * POST /api/admin/security/setup-2fa
 * Body: { password: string }
 * Response: { secret, otpauthUrl, recoveryCodes: string[] }
 *
 * Generates a new TOTP secret + 10 recovery codes.
 * NOTE: 2FA is NOT yet enabled — user must call /verify-2fa with a valid TOTP to finalize.
 */

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { password } = body as { password?: string };

    if (!password) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Password corrente richiesta per attivare 2FA" },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    const cred = await db.adminCredential.findUnique({
      where: { id: "singleton" },
    });
    if (!cred?.passwordHash) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Credenziali non inizializzate" },
        { status: 500 },
      );
    }

    const valid = await verifyPassword(password, cred.passwordHash);
    if (!valid) {
      await logAudit("2fa_setup_failed", undefined, { reason: "wrong_password" }, req);
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Password corrente non valida" },
        { status: 401 },
      );
    }

    const { secret, otpauthUrl } = generateTOTPSecret("admin@goldevents.it");

    const recoveryCodes: string[] = [];
    const hashedRecoveryCodes: string[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      const code = generateRecoveryCode();
      recoveryCodes.push(code);
      hashedRecoveryCodes.push(await bcrypt.hash(code, 10));
    }

    await db.adminCredential.update({
      where: { id: "singleton" },
      data: {
        totpSecret: secret,
        recoveryCodes: JSON.stringify(hashedRecoveryCodes),
      },
    });

    await logAudit("2fa_setup_initiated", undefined, undefined, req);

    return NextResponse.json({
      secret,
      otpauthUrl,
      recoveryCodes,
      message: "2FA setup avviato. Verifica con un codice TOTP per finalizzare.",
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Setup 2FA error:", error?.message);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Errore interno" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}
```

FILE: src/app/api/admin/security/verify-2fa/route.ts

```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { authenticate, logAudit } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { verifyTOTP } from "@/lib/auth-crypto";

/**
 * POST /api/admin/security/verify-2fa
 * Body: { token: string }
 *
 * Finalizes 2FA activation by verifying the first TOTP token from the user's authenticator app.
 * Sets totpEnrolledAt = now, marking 2FA as active.
 */

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { token } = body as { token?: string };

    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Token TOTP richiesto" },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    const cred = await db.adminCredential.findUnique({
      where: { id: "singleton" },
    });
    if (!cred?.totpSecret) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "2FA non inizializzato. Esegui prima /setup-2fa" },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    const valid = verifyTOTP(cred.totpSecret, token);
    if (!valid) {
      await logAudit("2fa_verify_failed", undefined, undefined, req);
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Token TOTP non valido" },
        { status: 401 },
      );
    }

    await db.adminCredential.update({
      where: { id: "singleton" },
      data: { totpEnrolledAt: new Date() },
    });

    await logAudit("2fa_enabled", undefined, undefined, req);

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      message: "2FA attivato con successo. Da ora ogni login richiederà il codice TOTP.",
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Verify 2FA error:", error?.message);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Errore interno" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}
```

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: src/app/api/admin/submissions/[id]/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  getSubmission,
  updateSubmission,
  deleteSubmission,
  logAuditEntry,
  kvAvailable,
} from "@/lib/kv-store";
import { authenticate } from "@/lib/admin-auth";

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  if (!kvAvailable) {
    return NextResponse.json({ submission: null });
  }
  const { id } = await params;
  const sub = await getSubmission(id);
  if (!sub) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Not found" }, { status: 404 });
  return NextResponse.json({ submission: sub });
}

export async function PATCH(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  if (!kvAvailable) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "KV not available" }, { status: 503 });
  }
  const { id } = await params;
  const body = await req.json();
  const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {};

  if (body.status !== undefined) updates.status = body.status;
  if (body.adminNotes !== undefined) updates.adminNotes = body.adminNotes;
  if (body.starred !== undefined) updates.starred = body.starred;
  if (body.budget !== undefined) updates.budget = body.budget;
  if (body.email !== undefined) updates.email = body.email;
  if (body.phone !== undefined) updates.phone = body.phone;

  // Handle reply tracking
  if (body.repliedVia !== undefined) {
    updates.repliedVia = body.repliedVia;
    updates.repliedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    if (body.repliedText !== undefined) updates.repliedText = body.repliedText;
    // Auto-update status to "contacted" when reply is sent
    if (body.status === undefined) updates.status = "contacted";
  }

  const updated = await updateSubmission(id, updates);
  if (!updated) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Not found" }, { status: 404 });
  }

  await logAuditEntry("update_submission", id, {
    status: body.status,
    starred: body.starred,
    repliedVia: body.repliedVia,
    budget: body.budget,
  }, { headers: req.headers });

  return NextResponse.json({ submission: updated });
}

export async function DELETE(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  if (!kvAvailable) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "KV not available" }, { status: 503 });
  }
  const { id } = await params;
  await deleteSubmission(id);
  await logAuditEntry("delete_submission", id, null, { headers: req.headers });
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
}
```

```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { authenticate } from "@/lib/admin-auth";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  try {
    const formData = await req.formData();
    const file = formData.get("file") as File | null;
    const placement = (formData.get("placement") as string) || "gallery";
    const title = (formData.get("title") as string) || "";

    if (!file) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Nessun file" }, { status: 400 });

    const MAX_SIZE = 4 * 1024 * 1024;
    if (file.size > MAX_SIZE) {
      return NextResponse.json({ 
        error: "File troppo grande (max 4MB). Per file grandi usa YouTube o Imgur." 
      }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const bytes = await file.arrayBuffer();
    const buffer = Buffer.from(bytes);
    const base64 = buffer.toString("base64");
    const mimeType = file.type || "image/jpeg";
    const dataUrl = `data:${mimeType};base64,${base64}`;
    const type = file.type.startsWith("video/") ? "video" : "image";

    const item = {
      id: `media_${Date.now()}`,
      type, url: dataUrl,
      title: title || file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, ""),
      placement,
      addedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    const globalStore = globalThis as unknown as { __mediaStore?: any[] };
    if (!globalStore.__mediaStore) globalStore.__mediaStore = [];
    globalStore.__mediaStore.unshift(item);

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, item, message: "Caricato! Visibile sul sito." });
  } catch (error: any) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Errore upload" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: src/app/api/admin/upload-large/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { authenticate } from "@/lib/admin-auth";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await authenticate(req))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  try {
    const formData = await req.formData();
    const file = formData.get("file") as File | null;
    const placement = (formData.get("placement") as string) || "gallery";
    const title = (formData.get("title") as string) || "";

    if (!file) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Nessun file" }, { status: 400 });

    const MAX_SIZE = 200 * 1024 * 1024;
    if (file.size > MAX_SIZE) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "File troppo grande (max 200MB). Per video grandi usa YouTube." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // Convert file to buffer
    const bytes = await file.arrayBuffer();
    const buffer = Buffer.from(bytes);

    // Try multiple upload services
    let uploadedUrl: string | null = null;

    // Try 1: 0x0.st
    try {
      const form = new FormData();
      form.append("file", new Blob([buffer], { type: file.type }), file.name);
      
      const res = await fetch("https://0x0.st", {
        method: "POST",
        body: form,
      });
      
      if (res.ok) {
        const text = await res.text();
        if (text.startsWith("https://")) {
          uploadedUrl = text.trim();
        }
      }
    } catch {}

    // Try 2: Catbox
    if (!uploadedUrl) {
      try {
        const form = new FormData();
        form.append("reqtype", "fileupload");
        form.append("fileToUpload", new Blob([buffer], { type: file.type }), file.name);
        
        const res = await fetch("https://catbox.moe/user/api.php", {
          method: "POST",
          body: form,
        });
        
        if (res.ok) {
          const text = await res.text();
          if (text.startsWith("https://")) {
            uploadedUrl = text.trim();
          }
        }
      } catch {}
    }

    // Try 3: tmpfiles.org
    if (!uploadedUrl) {
      try {
        const form = new FormData();
        form.append("file", new Blob([buffer], { type: file.type }), file.name);
        
        const res = await fetch("https://tmpfiles.org/api/v1/upload", {
          method: "POST",
          body: form,
        });
        
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.data?.url) {
            // Convert tmpfiles URL to direct link
            uploadedUrl = data.data.url.replace("tmpfiles.org/", "tmpfiles.org/dl/");
          }
        }
      } catch {}
    }

    // Fallback: base64 (for small files < 4MB)
    if (!uploadedUrl && file.size < 4 * 1024 * 1024) {
      const base64 = buffer.toString("base64");
      const mimeType = file.type || "image/jpeg";
      uploadedUrl = `data:${mimeType};base64,${base64}`;
    }

    if (!uploadedUrl) {
      return NextResponse.json({ 
        error: "Upload fallito. Prova con URL esterno (YouTube per video, Imgur per foto)." 
      }, { status: 500 });
    }

    const type = file.type.startsWith("video/") ? "video" : "image";
    const item = {
      id: `media_${Date.now()}`,
      type, url: uploadedUrl,
      title: title || file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, ""),
      placement,
      addedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    const globalStore = globalThis as unknown as { __mediaStore?: any[] };
    if (!globalStore.__mediaStore) globalStore.__mediaStore = [];
    globalStore.__mediaStore.unshift(item);

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true, item,
      message: "File caricato! Visibile sul sito.",
      sizeMB: Math.round(file.size / 1024 / 1024 * 10) / 10,
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Upload error:", error?.message);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Errore upload" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: src/app/api/chat/conversations/[id]/messages/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { getConversation, addMessage, updateConversation, kvAvailable } from "@/lib/kv-store";

const MAX_TEXT = 2000;

function clean(s: unknown, max: number): string {
  if (typeof s !== "string") return "";
  return s.trim().slice(0, max).replace(/[<>]/g, "");
}

/**
 * POST /api/chat/conversations/[id]/messages
 * Invia un messaggio del visitatore
 */
export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const text = clean(body.text, MAX_TEXT);

    if (!text) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Messaggio vuoto" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // Verifica che la conversazione appartenga al visitatore (via cookie)
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const cookieConvId = cookieStore.get("chat_conv_id")?.value;
    if (cookieConvId !== id) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Forbidden" }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const conv = await getConversation(id);
    if (!conv || conv.status === "closed") {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Conversazione chiusa o non trovata" },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    // Aggiorna metadati visitatore se forniti (su primo messaggio reale)
    const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {
      lastMessageAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      unreadByAdmin: conv.unreadByAdmin + 1,
    };
    if (body.visitorName && !conv.visitorName) {
      updates.visitorName = clean(body.visitorName, 100);
    }
    if (body.visitorEmail && !conv.visitorEmail) {
      updates.visitorEmail = clean(body.visitorEmail, 200);
    }
    if (body.visitorPhone && !conv.visitorPhone) {
      updates.visitorPhone = clean(body.visitorPhone, 50);
    }
    // Primo messaggio del visitatore - marca come "waiting" per Salvo
    if (conv.status === "active" && conv.unreadByAdmin === 0) {
      updates.status = "waiting";
    }

    await updateConversation(id, updates);

    // Crea il messaggio
    const msg = await addMessage(id, { role: "visitor", text });
    if (!msg) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Errore salvataggio" }, { status: 500 });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ message: msg });
  } catch (error: any) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: error?.message || "Server error" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}
```

FILE: src/app/api/chat/conversations/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import {
  createConversation,
  getConversationWithMessages,
  getMessages,
  updateConversation,
  markMessagesRead,
  kvAvailable,
} from "@/lib/kv-store";

function clean(s: unknown, max: number): string {
  if (typeof s !== "string") return "";
  return s.trim().slice(0, max).replace(/[<>]/g, "");
}

/**
 * POST /api/chat/conversations
 * Crea una nuova conversazione per il visitatore (o restituisce quella esistente via cookie)
 */
export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const visitorName = clean(body.visitorName, 100);
    const visitorEmail = clean(body.visitorEmail, 200);
    const visitorPhone = clean(body.visitorPhone, 50);

    const ip = req.headers.get("x-forwarded-for") || req.headers.get("x-real-ip") || "unknown";
    const userAgent = req.headers.get("user-agent") || "";

    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const existingConvId = cookieStore.get("chat_conv_id")?.value;

    // Se ha già una conversazione attiva, restituiscila
    if (existingConvId) {
      const existing = await getConversationWithMessages(existingConvId);
      if (existing && existing.conversation.status !== "closed") {
        return NextResponse.json({ conversation: existing.conversation, messages: existing.messages });
      }
    }

    // Crea nuova conversazione
    const conv = await createConversation(
      {
        visitorName: visitorName || null,
        visitorEmail: visitorEmail || null,
        visitorPhone: visitorPhone || null,
        visitorIp: String(ip).slice(0, 100),
        userAgent: userAgent.slice(0, 200),
        status: "active",
      },
      "Conversazione iniziata. Salvo ti risponderà appena possibile.",
    );

    if (!conv) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Errore creazione conversazione" }, { status: 500 });
    }

    const messages = await getMessages(conv.id);

    const res = NextResponse.json({ conversation: conv, messages });
    res.cookies.set("chat_conv_id", conv.id, {
      httpOnly: true,
      sameSite: "lax",
      maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 30, // 30 giorni
      path: "/",
    });
    return res;
  } catch (error: any) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: error?.message || "Server error" },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }
}

/**
 * GET /api/chat/conversations
 * Recupera la conversazione del visitatore (via cookie)
 */
export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const convId = cookieStore.get("chat_conv_id")?.value;
    if (!convId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ conversation: null });
    }
    const result = await getConversationWithMessages(convId);
    if (!result) {
      return NextResponse.json({ conversation: null });
    }

    // Reset unreadByVisitor quando il visitatore legge
    if (result.conversation.unreadByVisitor > 0) {
      await updateConversation(convId, { unreadByVisitor: 0 });
      await markMessagesRead(convId, "admin");
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      conversation: result.conversation,
      messages: result.messages,
    });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ conversation: null });
  }
}
```

```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
// DB imported dynamically inside handler
import ZAI from "z-ai-web-dev-sdk";

const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `Sei l'assistente virtuale di Gold Events by Salvo, brand di Salvo Piemonte con sede ad Adrano (Catania), Sicilia.

CONTESTO AZIENDALE:
- Salvo Piemonte è organizzatore di eventi con sede ad Adrano (CT)
- WhatsApp: +39 344 232 2176
- Email: goldeventssicilyexperience@outlook.com
- Instagram: @goldeventsicily_experience

9 SERVIZI PRINCIPALI (Gold Events by Salvo):
1. Battesimi, Comunioni e Cresime — Cerimonie familiari
2. Compleanni (18, 30, 40, 50+) — Divertimento puro
3. Matrimoni & Destination Wedding — Regia totale
4. Lauree — Feste giovanili
5. Feste Private — Anniversari, addii al celibato/nubilato
6. Feste a Tema — Anni '80/'90, Halloween, White Party
7. Feste Aziendali & Team Building — Premiazioni, meeting
8. Dj Set — Audio+luce professionale inclusi
9. Organizzazione Serate Giochi — Quiz, karaoke, cervelloni

3 BUNDLE DI CONDUZIONE:
- Party & Style: prezzo su richiesta (feste private, 18esimi, lauree)
- Family & Joy: prezzo su richiesta (battesimi, comunioni, cresime)
- Luxury Wedding: prezzo su richiesta (matrimoni, eventi gala) — PIÙ RICHIESTO

6 SERVIZI EXTRA (prezzi in abbinamento a un bundle):
- Air Mirror: +250€ (stampe illimitate) —origine 450€ se singolo
- 360 Video Booth Experience: +300€ (slow-motion per social)
- Video Guest Book Phone Experience: +80€ (telefono vintage)
- Set Scenografico Premium: +80€ (neon personalizzato)
- Ora Extra: +70€/h
- Fascia Trasferta: gratuito entro 30km Adrano, +40-60€ oltre

GOLD EVENTS SICILY EXPERIENCE (circuit turistico internazionale):
Salvo NON eroga direttamente questi servizi — fa da TRAMITE/curatore con partner locali selezionati:
- Etna Wine Tasting Premium (Milo, Castiglione, Randazzo, Linguaglossa)
- Etna Sunset Party
- Sicilian Food & Agriturismo (Adrano, Bronte, Nicolosi)
- Luxury Beach & Coast Tours (Taormina, Giardini Naxos, Letojanni)

REGOLE PER LE RISPOSTE:
- Sii breve (max 3-4 frasi), cortese, professionale, caloroso
- Parla in ITALIANO se l'utente scrive in italiano, in INGLESE se in inglese, ecc.
- Se l'utente chiede un preventivo specifico, suggerisci di compilare il modulo nella sezione Contatti del sito
- Se l'utente chiede info su prezzi dettagliati, dai i prezzi base sopra indicati
- NON inventare prezzi o servizi non elencati
- Se non sai qualcosa, suggerisci di contattare Salvo direttamente via WhatsApp
- Mantieni il tono luxury ma accessibile`;

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { message, sessionId, lang = "it" } = body as {
      message?: string;
      sessionId?: string;
      lang?: string;
    };

    if (!message || typeof message !== "string" || message.length > 1000) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Invalid message" },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    // Build or load chat session
    let session;
    let db: any;
    try { db = (await import("@/lib/db")).db; } catch { db = null; }
    let history: Array<{ role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string }> = [];
    if (sessionId) {
      try {
        session = await db.chatSession.findUnique({ where: { id: sessionId } });
        if (session) {
          history = JSON.parse(session.messages) as typeof history;
        }
      } catch {
        // ignore, will create new session
      }
    }

    const userMsg = { role: "user" as const, content: message };

    // Initialize ZAI SDK and call LLM
    const zai = await ZAI.create();
    const completion = await zai.chat.completions.create({
      messages: [
        { role: "system", content: SYSTEM_PROMPT },
        ...history.slice(-6), // keep last 6 messages for context
        userMsg,
      ],
      temperature: 0.6,
      maxTokens: 400,
    });

    const reply =
      completion.choices?.[0]?.message?.content ??
      "Mi dispiace, non sono riuscito a generare una risposta. Riprova tra un momento.";

    const assistantMsg = { role: "assistant" as const, content: reply };
    const newMessages = [...history, userMsg, assistantMsg].slice(-20); // cap history

    // Persist or update session — wrapped in try/catch for Vercel serverless
    const userAgent = req.headers.get("user-agent") ?? null;
    let sessionIdOut = sessionId;
    try {
      if (session) {
        await db.chatSession.update({
          where: { id: session.id },
          data: {
            messages: JSON.stringify(newMessages),
            lang,
            userAgent,
            updatedAt: new Date(),
          },
        });
        sessionIdOut = session.id;
      } else {
        const newSession = await db.chatSession.create({
          data: {
            messages: JSON.stringify(newMessages),
            lang,
            userAgent,
          },
        });
        sessionIdOut = newSession.id;
      }
    } catch {
      // DB not available on Vercel serverless — chat still works (no persistence)
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ reply, sessionId: sessionIdOut || "temp" });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Chat API error:", error?.message);
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        reply:
          "Ciao! Sono l'assistente di Gold Events by Salvo. Per informazioni contatta Salvo su WhatsApp +39 344 232 2176.",
      },
      { status: 200 },
    );
  }
}
```

FILE: src/app/api/contact/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
/**
 * Soluzione senza DB persistente:
 * - Le richieste del form vengono inoltrate via WhatsApp/Email a Salvo
 * - I dati NON vengono salvati (no DB persistente senza account esterno)
 * - L'admin vede un messaggio chiaro: "Per gestire le richieste in pannello, configura un DB"
 * - Ma il sistema FUNZIONA per il cliente: cliccando Invia apre WhatsApp con Salvo
 */

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { logAuditEntry, kvAvailable } from "@/lib/kv-store";

const MAX_LENGTHS = {
  fullName: 200, eventType: 50, eventDate: 20,
  location: 200, guests: 9999, notes: 2000,
};

function clean(s: unknown, maxLen: number): string {
  if (typeof s !== "string") return "";
  return s.trim().slice(0, maxLen).replace(/[<>]/g, "");
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();

    // Honeypot check
    if (body.website && typeof body.website === "string" && body.website.length > 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
    }

    const fullName = clean(body.fullName, MAX_LENGTHS.fullName);
    const eventType = clean(body.eventType, MAX_LENGTHS.eventType);
    const eventDate = clean(body.eventDate, MAX_LENGTHS.eventDate);
    const location = clean(body.location, MAX_LENGTHS.location);
    const notes = clean(body.notes, MAX_LENGTHS.notes);
    const email = clean(body.email, 200);
    const phone = clean(body.phone, 50);

    if (!fullName || fullName.length < 2) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Nome non valido" }, { status: 400 });
    if (!eventType) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Tipo di evento obbligatorio" }, { status: 400 });
    if (!eventDate) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Data obbligatoria" }, { status: 400 });
    if (!location) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Location obbligatoria" }, { status: 400 });

    const guests = Number(body.guests);
    if (!Number.isFinite(guests) || guests < 1 || guests > MAX_LENGTHS.guests) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Ospiti non validi" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    let services = "[]";
    if (Array.isArray(body.services)) {
      services = JSON.stringify(body.services.filter((s: any) => typeof s === "string").slice(0, 20));
    }

    // Tenta di salvare nel DB se disponibile
    if (kvAvailable) {
      try {
        const { createSubmission } = await import("@/lib/kv-store");
        await createSubmission({
          fullName, email: email || null, phone: phone || null,
          eventType, eventDate, location, guests, services, notes: notes || null,
        });
        await logAuditEntry(
          "notification_new_request",
          fullName,
          { fullName, eventType, eventDate, location, guests, services, notes: notes || "" },
          { headers: req.headers },
        );
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("Save failed, but form works (WhatsApp CTAs):", e);
      }
    }
    // Se il DB non è disponibile, il form FUNZIONA comunque perché il cliente
    // poi clicca "Invia via WhatsApp" o "Invia via Email" e parla con Salvo direttamente

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  }
}
```

```typescript
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getContentOverrides } from "@/lib/content-overrides";

/**
 * GET /api/content-overrides
 *
 * Public endpoint that returns the SiteContent overrides.
 * Used by client components (Bundles, Extras, Footer, etc.) to apply
 * AI-driven modifications to the public site.
 *
 * Response: { overrides: { "bundle.luxury-wedding.price": "700", ... } }
 */

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const overrides = await getContentOverrides();
    const response = NextResponse.json({ overrides });
    response.headers.set(
      "Cache-Control",
      "public, s-maxage=30, stale-while-revalidate=60",
    );
    return response;
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { overrides: {} },
      {
        status: 200,
        headers: { "Cache-Control": "public, s-maxage=30" },
      },
    );
  }
}
```

```typescript
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";

interface MediaItem {
  id: string; type: string; url: string; title: string; placement: string; addedAt: string;
}

const globalStore = globalThis as unknown as { __mediaStore?: MediaItem[] };

export async function GET() {
  let items = globalStore.__mediaStore || [];
  
  // If in-memory empty, try env var
  if (items.length === 0 && process.env.MEDIA_DATA) {
    try { items = JSON.parse(process.env.MEDIA_DATA); } catch {}
  }
  
  return NextResponse.json({ items });
}
```

FILE: src/app/api/partner/route.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createPartnerSubmission, logAuditEntry, kvAvailable } from "@/lib/kv-store";

const MAX_LENGTHS = {
  businessName: 200, activityType: 50, location: 200,
  referentName: 200, referentPhone: 30, referentEmail: 200,
  capacity: 200, b2bRates: 2000,
};

function clean(s: unknown, maxLen: number): string {
  if (typeof s !== "string") return "";
  return s.trim().slice(0, maxLen).replace(/[<>]/g, "");
}

const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
const PHONE_RE = /^[+\d\s().-]{6,30}$/;

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();

    if (body.website && typeof body.website === "string" && body.website.length > 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
    }

    const businessName = clean(body.businessName, MAX_LENGTHS.businessName);
    const activityType = clean(body.activityType, MAX_LENGTHS.activityType);
    const location = clean(body.location, MAX_LENGTHS.location);
    const referentName = clean(body.referentName, MAX_LENGTHS.referentName);
    const referentPhone = clean(body.referentPhone, MAX_LENGTHS.referentPhone);
    const referentEmail = clean(body.referentEmail, MAX_LENGTHS.referentEmail);
    const capacity = clean(body.capacity, MAX_LENGTHS.capacity);
    const b2bRates = clean(body.b2bRates, MAX_LENGTHS.b2bRates);
    const hasPower = body.hasPower === "si" || body.hasPower === "no" ? body.hasPower : "";

    if (!businessName || !activityType || !location || !referentName || !referentPhone || !referentEmail || !hasPower) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Tutti i campi obbligatori" }, { status: 400 });
    }
    if (!EMAIL_RE.test(referentEmail)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Email non valida" }, { status: 400 });
    }
    if (!PHONE_RE.test(referentPhone)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Telefono non valido" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    if (kvAvailable) {
      try {
        await createPartnerSubmission({
          businessName, activityType, location,
          referentName, referentPhone, referentEmail,
          capacity: capacity || null, hasPower,
          b2bRates: b2bRates || null,
        });
        await logAuditEntry(
          "notification_new_partner",
          businessName,
          { businessName, activityType, location },
          { headers: req.headers },
        );
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("KV save partner failed:", e);
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  }
}
```

```typescript
            <SectionHeading
              eyebrow="DOVE OPERO"

            />

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4">

                <StatCard label="Sede" value="Adrano (CT)" />
                <StatCard label="Trasferta gratis" value="30 km" />
              </div>
            </Reveal>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="border-t border-white/5 bg-[#0F0F0F] py-20 sm:py-24">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            {PROVINCE.map((provincia) => {
              const comuni = AREE.filter((a) => a.provincia === provincia);
              if (comuni.length === 0) return null;
              return (
                <div key={provincia} className="mb-14">

                          {comuni.length} comuni serviti
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>

function StatCard({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/20 bg-[#1C1C1C]/60 p-4 text-center">
      <p className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-3xl">
        {value}
      </p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-[#A1A1A1]">
        {label}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```typescript
function StatCard({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/20 bg-[#1C1C1C]/60 p-4 text-center">
      <p className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-2xl">{value}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-[#A1A1A1]">
        {label}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```typescript
                <Reveal key={tech.name} y={20} delay={0.05 * idx}>
                  <article className="h-full rounded-2xl border border-[#D4AF37]/20 bg-[#1C1C1C]/60 p-5">
                    <div className="mb-3 inline-flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/40 bg-[#D4AF37]/10">
                      <Sparkles className="size-5 text-[#D4AF37]" />
                    </div>
                    <h3 className="font-serif text-base font-semibold text-white">{tech.name}</h3>
                    <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#A1A1A1]">{tech.desc}</p>
                  </article>
                </Reveal>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* Comuni serviti */}
        <section className="border-t border-white/5 bg-[#0F0F0F] py-20 sm:py-24">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <div className="mb-10 text-center">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.24em] text-[#D4AF37]">
                DOVE OPERA
              </p>
              <h2 className="mt-2 font-serif text-3xl font-semibold text-white sm:text-4xl">
                Comuni serviti da Gold Eventi
              </h2>
              <p className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#A1A1A1]">
                Gold Eventi di Salvo Piemonte opera in tutta la Sicilia Orientale. Trasferta gratuita
                entro 30km da Adrano. Per eventi fuori Sicilia o all'estero, preventivo personalizzato.
              </p>
            </div>
            <Reveal y={20}>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
                {COMUNI_SERVITI.map((comune) => (
                  <span
                    key={comune}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-white/12 bg-[#1C1C1C]/60 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white/90"
                  >
                    {comune}
                  </span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </Reveal>
            <div className="mt-8 text-center">
              <Link
                href="/dove-opero"
                className="inline-flex h-11 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/60 px-6 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors hover:bg-[#D4AF37] hover:text-[#0F0F0F]"
              >
                Vedi tutti i comuni <ArrowRight className="size-4" />
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* CTA finale */}
        <section className="border-t border-white/5 bg-gradient-to-br from-[#1C1C1C] to-[#0F0F0F] py-20 sm:py-24">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 text-center sm:px-6">
            <Reveal y={20}>
              <h2 className="font-serif text-3xl font-semibold text-white sm:text-4xl">
                Vuoi organizzare un Gold Evento?
              </h2>
              <p className="mx-auto mt-4 max-w-xl text-base leading-relaxed text-[#A1A1A1]">
                Salvo Piemonte ti risponde entro 24 ore con una proposta personalizzata per il tuo
                evento in Sicilia. Scegli il canale che preferisci.
              </p>
              <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-3">
                <Link
                  href="/gold-events#contatti"
                  className="inline-flex h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] px-7 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#0F0F0F] transition-colors hover:bg-[#E5C75A]"
                >
                  Richiedi preventivo <ArrowRight className="size-4" />
                </Link>
                <a
                  href="https://wa.me/393442322176"
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="inline-flex h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/60 px-7 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors hover:bg-[#D4AF37] hover:text-[#0F0F0F]"
                >
                  <MessageCircle className="size-4" /> WhatsApp
                </a>
                <Link
                  href="/salvo-piemonte"
                  className="inline-flex h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-white/12 px-7 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#A1A1A1] transition-colors hover:border-[#D4AF37]/50 hover:text-white"
                >
                  Scopri Salvo Piemonte <ArrowRight className="size-4" />
                </Link>
              </div>
            </Reveal>
          </div>
        </section>
      </main>
      <Footer />
      <ChatbotWidget />
    </div>
  );
}

function StatCard({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/20 bg-[#1C1C1C]/60 p-4 text-center">
      <p className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-2xl">{value}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-[#A1A1A1]">
        {label}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}
```
