FILE: src/app/page.tsx
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { Sparkles, MapPin, ArrowRight, ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";
import Link from "next/link";

/**
 * Cinematic landing page — Hollywood-style intro + bivio.
 *
 * Flow:
 *  1. Intro animazione cinematografica (logo oro che si materializza, drappeggio scuro)
 *  2. Fade-in del bivio: due "mondi" affiancati
 *     - LEFT: Gold Events by Salvo (eventi locali, IT)
 *     - RIGHT: Gold Events Sicily Experience (turismo internazionale, IT/EN)
 *  3. User clicks one → navigate alla rispettiva sottopagina
 *
 * Le animazioni sono sequenziali: intro 0-3.5s, poi bivio 3.5s+.
 * Skip button in basso-destra per chi non vuole aspettare.
 */

export default function HomePage() {
  const [introComplete, setIntroComplete] = React.useState(false);
  const [skipIntro, setSkipIntro] = React.useState(false);

  // Auto-complete intro after 3.5s
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (skipIntro) {
      setIntroComplete(true);
      return;
    }
    const t = setTimeout(() => setIntroComplete(true), 3800);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [skipIntro]);

  return (
    <div className="relative min-h-screen overflow-hidden bg-[#0F0F0F] text-white">
      {/* === CINEMATIC INTRO OVERLAY === */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {!introComplete && <CinematicIntro onComplete={() => setIntroComplete(true)} />}
      </AnimatePresence>

      {/* === BIVIO (visibile dopo l'intro) === */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {introComplete && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            transition={{ duration: 1.2, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
            className="relative min-h-screen"
          >
            <Bivio />
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>

      {/* Skip button during intro */}
      {!introComplete && (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setSkipIntro(true)}
          className="fixed bottom-5 right-5 z-50 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/30 bg-[#0F0F0F]/80 px-4 py-2 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.24em] text-[#A1A1A1] backdrop-blur-sm transition-colors hover:border-[#D4AF37]/60 hover:text-[#D4AF37]"
        >
          Salta intro
          <ChevronDown className="size-3" />
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// =====================================
// Cinematic intro — Hollywood style
// =====================================
function CinematicIntro({ onComplete }: { onComplete: () => void }) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-40 flex items-center justify-center bg-[#0F0F0F]"
      initial={{ opacity: 1 }}
      exit={{ opacity: 0, transition: { duration: 1.0, ease: "easeInOut" } }}
    >
      {/* Animated gold particle field background */}
      <ParticleField />

      {/* Cinematic letterbox bars (top + bottom black bars that shrink like film) */}
      <motion.div
        className="absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-1/3 bg-black"
        initial={{ height: "50vh" }}
        animate={{ height: 0 }}
        transition={{ delay: 3.0, duration: 0.8, ease: [0.65, 0, 0.35, 1] }}
      />
      <motion.div
        className="absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-1/3 bg-black"
        initial={{ height: "50vh" }}
        animate={{ height: 0 }}
        transition={{ delay: 3.0, duration: 0.8, ease: [0.65, 0, 0.35, 1] }}
      />

      {/* Gold dust shimmer sweeping across */}
      <motion.div
        className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-[#D4AF37]/15 to-transparent"
        initial={{ x: "-100%" }}
        animate={{ x: "100%" }}
        transition={{ delay: 0.5, duration: 2.2, ease: "easeInOut" }}
      />

      {/* Logo materializzazione — sized-down for cinematic proportion */}
      <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col items-center gap-4 px-6 sm:gap-5">
        {/* Gold "GE" monogram in a circle */}
        <motion.div
          initial={{ scale: 0.4, opacity: 0, rotateZ: -8 }}
          animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1, rotateZ: 0 }}
          transition={{ delay: 0.3, duration: 1.4, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
          className="relative"
        >
          <div className="relative flex size-20 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 border-[#D4AF37]/60 sm:size-24">
            {/* Pulsing glow */}
            <motion.div
              className="absolute inset-0 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37]/30 blur-2xl"
              animate={{ opacity: [0.3, 0.7, 0.3] }}
              transition={{ duration: 2.5, repeat: Infinity, ease: "easeInOut" }}
            />
            {/* Inner rotating gold ring */}
            <motion.div
              className="absolute inset-2 rounded-full border border-dashed border-[#D4AF37]/40"
              animate={{ rotate: 360 }}
              transition={{ duration: 8, repeat: Infinity, ease: "linear" }}
            />
            <motion.span
              initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
              transition={{ delay: 0.9, duration: 0.6 }}
              className="font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-4xl"
            >
              GE
            </motion.span>
          </div>
        </motion.div>

        {/* Brand name */}
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 24 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ delay: 1.4, duration: 1.0, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
          className="text-center"
        >
          <h1 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white sm:text-3xl">
            GOLD EVENTS
          </h1>
          <motion.p
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            transition={{ delay: 1.9, duration: 0.8 }}
            className="mt-1.5 text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.5em] text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-[10px]"
          >
            by Salvo · Sicilia
          </motion.p>
        </motion.div>

        {/* Loading line that fills up */}
        <motion.div
          className="relative mt-6 h-px w-40 bg-white/10 sm:w-56"
          initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
          transition={{ delay: 2.2, duration: 0.5 }}
        >
          <motion.div
            className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 bg-[#D4AF37]"
            initial={{ width: "0%" }}
            animate={{ width: "100%" }}
            transition={{ delay: 2.4, duration: 1.2, ease: "easeInOut" }}
          />
        </motion.div>

        {/* Final tagline that fades in */}
        <motion.p
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 12 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ delay: 3.0, duration: 0.8 }}
          className="text-center text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.32em] text-[#A1A1A1] sm:text-sm"
        >
          Il Tuo Evento, Il Tuo Viaggio
        </motion.p>
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

// =====================================
// Animated particle field (gold dust)
// =====================================
function ParticleField() {
  // Pre-computed particle positions (deterministic for SSR-safety)
  const particles = React.useMemo(
    () =>
      Array.from({ length: 30 }).map((_, i) => ({
        id: i,
        x: Math.random() * 100,
        y: Math.random() * 100,
        size: 1 + Math.random() * 2.5,
        delay: Math.random() * 3,
        duration: 2 + Math.random() * 3,
      })),
    [],
  );

  return (
    <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden">
      {particles.map((p) => (
        <motion.span
          key={p.id}
          className="absolute rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37]"
          style={{
            left: `${p.x}%`,
            top: `${p.y}%`,
            width: p.size,
            height: p.size,
          }}
          initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
          animate={{
            opacity: [0, 0.8, 0],
            y: [0, -20, -40],
            x: [0, p.id % 2 === 0 ? 10 : -10, 0],
          }}
          transition={{
            delay: p.delay,
            duration: p.duration,
            repeat: Infinity,
            ease: "easeInOut",
          }}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

// =====================================

// =====================================
// World card — full-screen entry point
// =====================================
function WorldCard({
  variant,
  image,
  icon,
  eyebrow,
  title,
  subtitle,
  description,
  languages,
  href,
  cta,
  delay,
}: {
  variant: "local" | "international";
  image: string;
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  eyebrow: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  description: string;
  languages: string;
  href: string;
  cta: string;
  delay: number;
}) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 40 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ delay, duration: 0.8, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
      className="group relative flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#D4AF37]/30 bg-[#1C1C1C]/80 transition-colors hover:border-[#D4AF37]/60"
    >
      <Link href={href} prefetch={true} className="flex size-full flex-col" aria-label={`${title} ${subtitle}`}>
        {/* === Cover image — 3:4 portrait (foto intera senza taglio), contained, no overflow === */}
        <div className="relative aspect-[3/4] w-full shrink-0 overflow-hidden bg-[#0F0F0F]">
          {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
          <img
            src={image}
            alt={`${title} ${subtitle}`}
            className="size-full object-cover transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:scale-105"
          />
          {/* Variant badge overlayed on image */}
          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute right-2.5 top-2.5 z-10">
            <span className="rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/40 bg-[#0F0F0F]/85 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#D4AF37] backdrop-blur-sm sm:text-[10px]">
              {variant === "international" ? "IT / EN" : "IT"}
            </span>
          </div>
          {/* Bottom gradient fade into card body for legibility */}
          <div
            aria-hidden
            className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-12 bg-gradient-to-t from-[#1C1C1C] to-transparent"
          />
        </div>

        {/* === Card body — text content === */}
        <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col p-4 sm:p-5">
          {/* Icon */}
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.6 }}
            whileInView={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
            transition={{ delay: delay + 0.3, duration: 0.5 }}
            className="mb-2.5 inline-flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/50 bg-[#D4AF37]/10 text-[#D4AF37] sm:size-10"
          >
            {icon}
          </motion.div>

          {/* Eyebrow */}
          <p className="text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.28em] text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-[10px]">
            {eyebrow}
          </p>

          {/* Title */}
          <h2 className="mt-1.5 font-serif text-lg font-bold leading-tight text-white sm:text-xl">
            {title}
          </h2>
          <p className="font-serif text-base font-light italic text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-lg">
            {subtitle}
          </p>

          {/* Description */}
          <p className="mt-2 flex-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-[#A1A1A1] sm:text-sm">
            {description}
          </p>

          {/* CTA */}
          <div className="mt-3 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-[11px]">
            {cta}
            <ArrowRight
              className="size-3 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1"
              strokeWidth={2.5}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </Link>

      {/* Gold hairline that animates on hover (outside Link, doesn't block clicks) */}
      <div
        aria-hidden
        className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-px bg-[#D4AF37] opacity-0 transition-opacity duration-500 group-hover:opacity-100"
      />
    </motion.div>
  );
}
```

```typescript
function StatCard({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/20 bg-[#1C1C1C]/60 p-4 text-center">
      <p className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-2xl">{value}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-[#A1A1A1]">
        {label}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```typescript
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { Header } from "@/components/site/header";
import { SicilyHero } from "@/components/site/sicily-hero";
import { BivioBack } from "@/components/site/bivio-back";
import { Footer } from "@/components/site/footer";
import { SicilyExperience } from "@/components/site/sicily-experience";
import { ContactForm } from "@/components/site/contact-form";
import { PartnerForm } from "@/components/site/partner-form";
import { ChatbotWidget } from "@/components/site/chatbot-widget";
import { MediaDisplay } from "@/components/site/media-display";

const BASE_URL = "https://goldeventsbysalvo.vercel.app";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Destination Sicily · Esperienze di Lusso in Sicilia · IT / EN",
  description:
    "Destination weddings, tour sull'Etna, degustazioni in cantina, agriturismo e beach party a Taormina. Salvo Piemonte curatore di esperienze di lusso in Sicilia Orientale. Italiano · English.",
  alternates: {
    canonical: BASE_URL + "/sicily-experience",
  },
  openGraph: {
    title: "Gold Events Sicily Experience · Destination Sicily",
    description:
      "Esperienze indimenticabili in Sicilia Orientale: Etna, Taormina, degustazioni vini, agriturismo, beach party. Curate da Salvo Piemonte.",
    url: BASE_URL + "/sicily-experience",
    images: ["/images/collage/sicily-aerial-etna-taormina.jpg"],
  },
};

export default function SicilyExperiencePage() {
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col bg-[#0F0F0F]">
      <Header />
      <main className="flex-1">
        <BivioBack
          currentLabel="Gold Events Sicily Experience"
          otherHref="/gold-events"
          otherLabel="Gold Events by Salvo"
        />
        <SicilyHero />
        <SicilyExperience />
        <MediaDisplay />
        <ContactForm />
        <PartnerForm />
      </main>
      <Footer />
      <ChatbotWidget />
    </div>
  );
}
```

FILE: src/app/sitemap.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
import { MetadataRoute } from "next";

const BASE_URL = "https://goldeventsbysalvo.vercel.app";
const LANGS = [
  "it",
  "en",
  "fr",
  "de",
  "es",
  "pt",
  "nl",
  "ru",
  "ar",
  "zh",
  "ja",
  "ko",
  "pl",
  "tr",
  "hi",
];

const SECTIONS = [
  { path: "/", priority: 1.0, changeFreq: "weekly" as const },
  { path: "/eventi", priority: 0.99, changeFreq: "weekly" as const },
  { path: "/gold-eventi", priority: 0.98, changeFreq: "weekly" as const },
  { path: "/salvo-piemonte", priority: 0.98, changeFreq: "weekly" as const },
  { path: "/gold-events", priority: 0.95, changeFreq: "weekly" as const },
  { path: "/sicily-experience", priority: 0.95, changeFreq: "weekly" as const },
  { path: "/dove-opero", priority: 0.92, changeFreq: "monthly" as const },
  { path: "/prezzi", priority: 0.92, changeFreq: "monthly" as const },
  { path: "/recensioni", priority: 0.88, changeFreq: "monthly" as const },
  { path: "/faq", priority: 0.88, changeFreq: "monthly" as const },
];

export default function sitemap(): MetadataRoute.Sitemap {
  const now = new Date();
  return SECTIONS.map((s) => ({
    url: `${BASE_URL}${s.path}`,
    lastModified: now,
    changeFrequency: s.changeFreq,
    priority: s.priority,
    alternates: {
      languages: Object.fromEntries(
        LANGS.map((l) => [l, `${BASE_URL}${s.path}`]),
      ),
    },
  }));
}
```

FILE: src/components/site/bivio-back.tsx
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, Repeat } from "lucide-react";

/**
 * Sticky banner at the top of subpages — shows current world + link to switch.
 * Lets the user jump from /gold-events to /sicily-experience and vice versa
 * without going back to the bivio landing page.
 */
export function BivioBack({
  currentLabel,
  otherHref,
  otherLabel,
}: {
  currentLabel: string;
  otherHref: string;
  otherLabel: string;
}) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -12 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}
      className="sticky top-16 z-30 border-b border-[#D4AF37]/15 bg-[#0F0F0F]/95 backdrop-blur-xl sm:top-20"
    >
      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-3 sm:px-6">
        <Link
          href="/"
          prefetch={true}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-[#A1A1A1] transition-colors hover:text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-xs"
        >
          <ArrowLeft className="size-3.5" />
          <span className="hidden sm:inline">Torna al bivio</span>
          <span className="sm:hidden">Bivio</span>
        </Link>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-xs">
          <Repeat className="size-3.5" />
          <span className="hidden sm:inline">Stai visitando:</span>
          <span className="truncate">{currentLabel}</span>
        </div>

        <Link
          href={otherHref}
          prefetch={true}
          className="group inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-white transition-colors hover:text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-xs"
        >
          <span className="hidden truncate sm:inline">{otherLabel}</span>
          <span className="sm:hidden">Altro</span>
          <ArrowRight className="size-3.5 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1" />
        </Link>
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}
```

```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { Check, ArrowRight, Star } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { SectionHeading } from "@/components/site/section-heading";
import { StaggerGroup, itemVariants } from "@/components/site/reveal";
import { bundles, type Bundle } from "@/lib/site-content";
import { usePreferredBundle } from "@/lib/use-preferred-bundle";
import { useDict } from "@/lib/i18n/use-lang";
import { useContentOverrides } from "@/lib/use-content-overrides";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export function Bundles() {
  const t = useDict();
  const { overrides } = useContentOverrides();

  // Apply price overrides to bundles (AI can change prices from admin panel)
  const bundlesWithOverrides = bundles.map((bundle) => {
    const overrideKey = `bundle.${bundle.id}.price`;
    const overrideValue = overrides[overrideKey];
    if (overrideValue) {
      const num = parseInt(overrideValue.replace(/[^\d]/g, ""), 10);
      if (Number.isFinite(num) && num > 0) {
        return { ...bundle, price: num };
      }
    }
    return bundle;
  });

  return (
    <section
      id="bundles"
      aria-labelledby="bundles-title"
      className="relative scroll-mt-24 py-20 sm:py-28"
    >
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <SectionHeading
          eyebrow={t.eyebrow.bundles}
          title={t.bundlesTitle}
          intro={t.bundlesIntro}
        />

        <StaggerGroup
          className="mt-14 grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-3 md:items-stretch"
          stagger={0.1}
        >
          {bundlesWithOverrides.map((bundle) => (
            <BundleCard
              key={bundle.id}
              bundle={bundle}
              badgeLabel={
                bundle.id === "family-joy"
                  ? t.bundleBadgeFamilyJoy
                  : bundle.id === "luxury-wedding"
                    ? t.bundleBadgeLuxury
                    : bundle.badge
              }
              ctaLabel={t.bundlesCta}
              priceFromLabel={t.bundlesPriceFrom}
            />
          ))}
        </StaggerGroup>

        <p className="mt-8 text-center text-xs text-[#A1A1A1]">
          {t.bundlesFooterNote}
        </p>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function BundleCard({
  bundle,
  badgeLabel,
  ctaLabel,
  priceFromLabel,
}: {
  bundle: Bundle;
  badgeLabel?: string;
  ctaLabel: string;
  priceFromLabel: string;
}) {
  const setPreferredBundle = usePreferredBundle((s) => s.setPreferredBundle);

  const handleSelect = () => {
    setPreferredBundle(bundle.id);
    toast.success(`${bundle.name} preselezionato`, {
      description: "Ti aspettiamo nel modulo di contatto.",
    });
    document
      .querySelector("#contatti")
      ?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
  };

  const highlighted = bundle.highlighted;

  return (
    <motion.article
      variants={itemVariants}
      className={cn(
        "relative flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl p-7 transition-all duration-300",
        highlighted
          ? "border-2 border-[#D4AF37] bg-gradient-to-b from-[#1C1C1C] to-[#0F0F0F] shadow-[0_24px_60px_-20px_rgba(212,175,55,0.45)] md:-translate-y-3"
          : "border border-white/8 bg-[#1C1C1C]/70 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-[#D4AF37]/40",
      )}
    >
      {highlighted && (
        <span
          aria-hidden
          className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 -top-px h-px bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-[#D4AF37] to-transparent"
        />
      )}

      {/* Badge */}
      {badgeLabel && (
        <span
          className={cn(
            "absolute right-5 top-5 inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em]",
            highlighted
              ? "bg-[#D4AF37] text-[#0F0F0F]"
              : "border border-[#D4AF37]/40 text-[#D4AF37]",
          )}
        >
          {highlighted && <Star className="size-3 fill-current" />}
          {badgeLabel}
        </span>
      )}

      <div className="flex-1">
        <h3 className="font-serif text-2xl font-semibold text-white sm:text-3xl">
          {bundle.name}
        </h3>
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-[#A1A1A1]">{bundle.audience}</p>

        {bundle.duration && (
          <p className="mt-3 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#D4AF37]">
            <Check className="size-3.5" strokeWidth={2.5} />
            {bundle.duration}
          </p>
        )}

        <p className="mt-5 text-sm leading-relaxed text-white/85">
          {bundle.description}
        </p>

        {/* Itemized inclusions */}
        <ul className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-2.5">
          {bundle.inclusions.map((inc, idx) => (
            <li key={idx} className="flex items-start gap-2.5 text-sm text-white/85">
              <Check
                className={cn(
                  "mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0",
                  highlighted ? "text-[#D4AF37]" : "text-[#D4AF37]/80",
                )}
                strokeWidth={2.5}
              />
              <span className="leading-snug">{inc}</span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>

        <div className="mt-7 flex items-baseline gap-2">
          {bundle.showPrice === false ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <span className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#A1A1A1]">
                Prezzo
              </span>
              <span className="font-serif text-xl font-semibold text-gold-gradient sm:text-2xl">
                Su richiesta
              </span>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-[#6B6B6B]">
                Preventivo personalizzato in base ai servizi
              </span>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <span className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#A1A1A1]">
                {priceFromLabel}
              </span>
              <span className="font-serif text-4xl font-bold text-gold-gradient sm:text-5xl">
                {bundle.price}€
              </span>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={handleSelect}
        className={cn(
          "mt-7 inline-flex h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] transition-all",
          highlighted
            ? "bg-[#D4AF37] text-[#0F0F0F] hover:bg-[#E5C75A]"
            : "border border-[#D4AF37]/60 text-[#D4AF37] hover:bg-[#D4AF37] hover:text-[#0F0F0F]",
        )}
      >
        {ctaLabel}
        <ArrowRight className="size-3.5" strokeWidth={2} />
      </button>
    </motion.article>
  );
}
```

```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { MessageCircle, X, Send, Loader2, Headphones, Bot } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { useLang } from "@/lib/i18n/use-lang";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { HumanChatWidget } from "./human-chat-widget";

interface Message {
  role: "user" | "assistant";
  content: string;
}

const WELCOME_MESSAGES: Record<string, string> = {
  it: "Ciao! Sono l'assistente virtuale di Gold Events by Salvo. Posso aiutarti a scegliere il servizio giusto, capire i prezzi, o pianificare il tuo evento. Come posso aiutarti?",
  en: "Hi! I'm the virtual assistant of Gold Events by Salvo. I can help you choose the right service, understand pricing, or plan your event. How can I help you?",
  fr: "Bonjour ! Je suis l'assistant virtuel de Gold Events by Salvo. Je peux vous aider à choisir le bon service, comprendre les prix, ou planifier votre événement. Comment puis-je vous aider ?",
  de: "Hallo! Ich bin der virtuelle Assistent von Gold Events by Salvo. Ich kann Ihnen helfen, die richtige Service zu wählen, Preise zu verstehen oder Ihr Event zu planen. Wie kann ich helfen?",
  es: "¡Hola! Soy el asistente virtual de Gold Events by Salvo. Puedo ayudarte a elegir el servicio adecuado, entender los precios o planificar tu evento. ¿Cómo puedo ayudarte?",
  pt: "Olá! Sou o assistente virtual da Gold Events by Salvo. Posso ajudá-lo a escolher o serviço certo, entender os preços ou planejar o seu evento. Como posso ajudar?",
};

const FALLBACK_WELCOME =
  "Hi! I'm the Gold Events by Salvo assistant. How can I help you with your event?";

export function ChatbotWidget() {
  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const [messages, setMessages] = React.useState<Message[]>([]);
  const [input, setInput] = React.useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(false);
  const [sessionId, setSessionId] = React.useState<string | null>(null);
  const [hasInteracted, setHasInteracted] = React.useState(false);
  const [mode, setMode] = React.useState<"ai" | "human">("ai");

  const { lang } = useLang();
  const scrollRef = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const inputRef = React.useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  // Welcome message when first opened
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (open && messages.length === 0) {
      setMessages([
        {
          role: "assistant",
          content: WELCOME_MESSAGES[lang] ?? FALLBACK_WELCOME,
        },
      ]);
    }
  }, [open, lang, messages.length]);

  // Auto-scroll to bottom on new message
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (scrollRef.current) {
      scrollRef.current.scrollTop = scrollRef.current.scrollHeight;
    }
  }, [messages, loading]);

  // Focus input when opened
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (open && inputRef.current) {
      setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 300);
    }
  }, [open]);

  const send = async () => {
    const text = input.trim();
    if (!text || loading) return;

    setInput("");
    setLoading(true);
    setHasInteracted(true);
    setMessages((prev) => [...prev, { role: "user", content: text }]);

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/chat", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          message: text,
          sessionId,
          lang,
        }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.sessionId) setSessionId(data.sessionId);
      setMessages((prev) => [
        ...prev,
        { role: "assistant", content: data.reply || "(nessuna risposta)" },
      ]);
    } catch (error) {
      setMessages((prev) => [
        ...prev,
        {
          role: "assistant",
          content:
            lang === "it"
              ? "Mi dispiace, c'è stato un errore. Riprova tra un momento, oppure contatta Salvo direttamente su WhatsApp +39 344 232 2176."
              : "Sorry, there was an error. Please try again in a moment, or contact Salvo directly on WhatsApp +39 344 232 2176.",
        },
      ]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      {/* Floating button */}
      <motion.button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
        aria-label={open ? "Chiudi chat" : "Apri chat assistente"}
        className="fixed bottom-5 right-5 z-50 inline-flex size-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] text-[#0F0F0F] shadow-[0_10px_30px_-5px_rgba(212,175,55,0.6)] transition-all hover:scale-105 hover:bg-[#E5C75A] sm:bottom-6 sm:right-6 sm:size-16"
        initial={{ scale: 0, opacity: 0 }}
        animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}
        transition={{ delay: 1.2, type: "spring", stiffness: 200, damping: 18 }}
      >
        {/* Pulsing ring to draw attention */}
        {!hasInteracted && !open && (
          <span
            aria-hidden
            className="absolute inset-0 -z-10 animate-ping rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37]/40"
            style={{ animationDuration: "2.5s" }}
          />
        )}
        <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
          {open ? (
            <motion.span
              key="x"
              initial={{ rotate: -90, opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ rotate: 0, opacity: 1 }}
              exit={{ rotate: 90, opacity: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
            >
              <X className="size-6" strokeWidth={2.5} />
            </motion.span>
          ) : (
            <motion.span
              key="msg"
              initial={{ rotate: 90, opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ rotate: 0, opacity: 1 }}
              exit={{ rotate: -90, opacity: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
            >
              <MessageCircle className="size-6" strokeWidth={2.2} />
            </motion.span>
          )}
        </AnimatePresence>
      </motion.button>

      {/* Chat panel */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {open && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 30, scale: 0.95 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: 30, scale: 0.95 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.25, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
            className="fixed bottom-24 right-3 z-50 flex h-[520px] w-[calc(100vw-1.5rem)] max-w-[400px] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#D4AF37]/30 bg-[#0F0F0F] shadow-[0_30px_80px_-20px_rgba(0,0,0,0.9)] sm:bottom-28 sm:right-6"
          >
            {mode === "human" ? (
              <HumanChatWidget onBackToBot={() => setMode("ai")} />
            ) : (
              <>
                {/* Header */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-white/8 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#1C1C1C] to-[#0F0F0F] px-4 py-3.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <span className="inline-flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/40 bg-[#D4AF37]/10 text-[#D4AF37]">
                      <Bot className="size-4" strokeWidth={2} />
                    </span>
                    <div className="flex flex-col leading-tight">
                      <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">
                        Assistente Gold Events
                      </span>
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-[#D4AF37]/80">
                        <span className="size-1.5 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-green-400" />
                        Online
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setMode("human")}
                      aria-label="Parla con Salvo"
                      title="Parla con Salvo (chat umana)"
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/30 bg-[#D4AF37]/10 px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors hover:bg-[#D4AF37] hover:text-[#0F0F0F]"
                    >
                      <Headphones className="size-3" />
                      Salvo
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                      aria-label="Chiudi"
                      className="rounded-full p-1.5 text-white/60 transition-colors hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-white"
                    >
                      <X className="size-4" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Messages */}
                <div
                  ref={scrollRef}
                  className="gold-scroll flex-1 space-y-3 overflow-y-auto p-4"
                >
                  {messages.map((m, idx) => (
                    <div
                      key={idx}
                      className={cn(
                        "flex",
                        m.role === "user" ? "justify-end" : "justify-start",
                      )}
                    >
                      <div
                        className={cn(
                          "max-w-[85%] whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm leading-relaxed",
                          m.role === "user"
                            ? "rounded-br-md bg-[#D4AF37] text-[#0F0F0F]"
                            : "rounded-bl-md border border-white/8 bg-[#1C1C1C] text-white/90",
                        )}
                      >
                        {m.content}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                  {loading && (
                    <div className="flex justify-start">
                      <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl rounded-bl-md border border-white/8 bg-[#1C1C1C] px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm text-[#A1A1A1]">
                        <Loader2 className="size-3.5 animate-spin text-[#D4AF37]" />
                        <span>Sto pensando…</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Input */}
                <div className="border-t border-white/8 bg-[#0F0F0F] p-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <input
                      ref={inputRef}
                      type="text"
                      value={input}
                      onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
                      onKeyDown={(e) => {
                        if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) {
                          e.preventDefault();
                          send();
                        }
                      }}
                      placeholder="Scrivi un messaggio…"
                      maxLength={500}
                      className="h-10 flex-1 rounded-full border border-white/12 bg-[#1C1C1C] px-4 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-[#6B6B6B] focus:border-[#D4AF37] focus:outline-none focus:ring-0"
                    />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={send}
                      disabled={!input.trim() || loading}
                      aria-label="Invia"
                      className="inline-flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] text-[#0F0F0F] transition-colors hover:bg-[#E5C75A] disabled:opacity-40 disabled:hover:bg-[#D4AF37]"
                    >
                      <Send className="size-4" strokeWidth={2.2} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-center text-[10px] text-[#6B6B6B]">
                    Assistente AI · Per parlare con Salvo clicca "Salvo"
                  </p>
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </>
  );
}
```

```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { z } from "zod";
import { Loader2, Send, Check, CalendarDays, Square, CheckSquare, MessageCircle, Mail } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import {
  Form,
  FormControl,
  FormDescription,
  FormField,
  FormItem,
  FormLabel,
  FormMessage,
} from "@/components/ui/form";
import { SectionHeading } from "@/components/site/section-heading";
import { Reveal } from "@/components/site/reveal";
import {
  eventTypeOptions,
  serviceCheckboxes,
  contactInfo,
} from "@/lib/site-content";
import { usePreferredBundle } from "@/lib/use-preferred-bundle";

const formSchema = z.object({
  fullName: z
    .string()
    .min(2, "Inserisci il tuo nome o la ragione sociale."),
  email: z
    .string()
    .email("Email non valida.")
    .optional()
    .or(z.literal("")),
  phone: z
    .string()
    .min(6, "Inserisci un telefono valido (minimo 6 cifre).")
    .optional()
    .or(z.literal("")),
  eventType: z.string({ required_error: "Seleziona il tipo di evento." }),
  eventDate: z.string({ required_error: "Seleziona una data." }),
  location: z
    .string()
    .min(2, "Indica una location o una città di preferenza."),
  guests: z.coerce
    .number({ required_error: "Indica un numero stimato di ospiti." })
    .int("Deve essere un numero intero.")
    .min(1, "Almeno un ospite."),
  services: z
    .array(z.string())
    .min(1, "Seleziona almeno un servizio desiderato."),
  notes: z.string().optional(),
}).refine((data) => data.email || data.phone, {
  message: "Inserisci almeno un recapito (email o telefono).",
  path: ["phone"],
});

type FormValues = z.infer<typeof formSchema>;

const bundleLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  "party-style": "Party & Style",
  "family-joy": "Family & Joy",
  "luxury-wedding": "Luxury Wedding",
};

export function ContactForm() {
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = React.useState(false);
  const [submittedValues, setSubmittedValues] = React.useState<FormValues | null>(null);
  const preferredBundle = usePreferredBundle((s) => s.preferredBundle);
  const signal = usePreferredBundle((s) => s.signal);
  const clearPreferred = usePreferredBundle((s) => s.clear);

  // Honeypot field — bots fill hidden inputs, humans don't see them
  const [honeypotWebsite, setHoneypotWebsite] = React.useState("");

  const form = useForm<FormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(formSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      fullName: "",
      email: "",
      phone: "",
      eventType: undefined,
      eventDate: "",
      location: "",
      guests: undefined,
      services: [],
      notes: "",
    },
    mode: "onTouched",
  });

  const {
    handleSubmit,
    control,
    reset,
    setValue,
    watch,
    formState: { isSubmitting, errors },
  } = form;

  const watchedServices = watch("services");

  // Apply bundle preselection whenever signal bumps.
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!preferredBundle) return;
    const label = bundleLabels[preferredBundle];
    if (!label) return;

    // Preselect the matching "Conduzione Base — X" checkbox.
    const found = serviceCheckboxes.find((s) =>
      s.label.toLowerCase().includes(label.toLowerCase()),
    );
    const current = form.getValues("services") ?? [];
    if (found && !current.includes(found.id)) {
      setValue("services", [...current, found.id], {
        shouldValidate: false,
        shouldDirty: true,
      });
    }

    toast.success(`Conduzione "${label}" preselezionata`, {
      description: "Hai un passo in meno da fare nel modulo.",
    });

    // Clear so we don't re-apply if user toggles things.
    clearPreferred();
  }, [signal]);

  const onSubmit = async (values: FormValues) => {
    // Honeypot check — if filled, silently pretend success
    if (honeypotWebsite) {
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));
      setSubmittedValues(values);
      setSubmitted(true);
      return;
    }

    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 600));

    // Save to DB in background (don't block user if API fails — WhatsApp is the primary channel)
    fetch("/api/contact", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ ...values, website: honeypotWebsite }),
    }).catch(() => {
      // ignore — WhatsApp/email CTAs still work even if DB write fails
    });

    setSubmittedValues(values);
    setSubmitted(true);
    toast.success("Richiesta pronta per l'invio!", {
      description: "Scegli come inviarla: WhatsApp o Email.",
    });
    reset();
    setHoneypotWebsite("");
  };

  const onError = () => {
    toast.error("Controlla i campi evidenziati", {
      description: "Alcuni campi obbligatori non sono stati compilati.",
    });
  };

  return (
    <section
      id="contatti"
      aria-labelledby="contact-title"
      className="relative scroll-mt-24 border-t border-white/5 bg-[#0F0F0F] py-20 sm:py-28"
    >
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <SectionHeading
          eyebrow="RICHIESTA PREVENTIVO"
          title="Parliamo Del Tuo Evento"
          intro="Compila il modulo: ti rispondo entro 24 ore con una proposta personalizzata."
        />

        <Reveal y={28} delay={0.05} className="mt-12">
          <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-[#D4AF37]/20 bg-[#1C1C1C]/60 p-5 shadow-[0_30px_80px_-40px_rgba(0,0,0,0.8)] sm:p-8">
            <div
              aria-hidden
              className="pointer-events-none absolute -top-24 -right-24 size-48 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37]/10 blur-3xl"
            />

            {submitted ? (
              <SuccessState
                values={submittedValues}
                onReset={() => {
                  setSubmitted(false);
                  setSubmittedValues(null);
                }}
              />
            ) : (
              <Form {...form}>
                <form
                  onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit, onError)}
                  className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2"
                  noValidate
                >
                  <FormField
                    control={control}
                    name="fullName"
                    render={({ field }) => (
                      <FormItem className="sm:col-span-2">
                        <FormLabel className="text-white">
                          Nome e Cognome / Nome Azienda
                        </FormLabel>
                        <FormControl>
                          <Input
                            {...field}
                            placeholder="Mario Rossi / Azienda S.r.l."
                            className={fieldClasses(!!errors.fullName)}
                          />
                        </FormControl>
                        <FormMessage />
                      </FormItem>
                    )}
                  />

                  <FormField
                    control={control}
                    name="email"
                    render={({ field }) => (
                      <FormItem>
                        <FormLabel className="text-white">
                          Email <span className="text-[#A1A1A1]">(se non lasci il telefono)</span>
                        </FormLabel>
                        <FormControl>
                          <Input
                            {...field}
                            type="email"
                            placeholder="mario.rossi@email.com"
                            className={cn(fieldClasses(!!errors.email), "h-11")}
                          />
                        </FormControl>
                        <FormMessage />
                      </FormItem>
                    )}
                  />

                  <FormField
                    control={control}
                    name="phone"
                    render={({ field }) => (
                      <FormItem>
                        <FormLabel className="text-white">
                          Telefono / WhatsApp <span className="text-[#A1A1A1]">(se non lasci l'email)</span>
                        </FormLabel>
                        <FormControl>
                          <Input
                            {...field}
                            type="tel"
                            placeholder="+39 333 1234567"
                            className={cn(fieldClasses(!!errors.phone), "h-11")}
                          />
                        </FormControl>
                        <FormMessage />
                      </FormItem>
                    )}
                  />

                  <FormField
                    control={control}
                    name="eventType"
                    render={({ field }) => (
                      <FormItem>
                        <FormLabel className="text-white">
                          Tipo di Evento
                        </FormLabel>
                        <Select
                          value={field.value}
                          onValueChange={field.onChange}
                        >
                          <FormControl>
                            <SelectTrigger
                              className={cn(
                                "w-full bg-transparent",
                                fieldClasses(!!errors.eventType),
                                "h-11",
                              )}
                            >
                              <SelectValue placeholder="Seleziona il tipo" />
                            </SelectTrigger>
                          </FormControl>
                          <SelectContent className="border-[#D4AF37]/20 bg-[#1C1C1C] text-white">
                            {eventTypeOptions.map((opt) => (
                              <SelectItem key={opt} value={opt}>
                                {opt}
                              </SelectItem>
                            ))}
                          </SelectContent>
                        </Select>
                        <FormMessage />
                      </FormItem>
                    )}
                  />

                  <FormField
                    control={control}
                    name="eventDate"
                    render={({ field }) => (
                      <FormItem>
                        <FormLabel className="text-white">
                          Data dell'Evento
                        </FormLabel>
                        <FormControl>
                          <div className="relative">
                            <Input
                              type="date"
                              {...field}
                              className={cn(
                                fieldClasses(!!errors.eventDate),
                                "h-11 pr-10 [color-scheme:dark]",
                              )}
                            />
                            <CalendarDays
                              className="pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 right-3 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#D4AF37]/70"
                              aria-hidden
                            />
                          </div>
                        </FormControl>
                        <FormMessage />
                      </FormItem>
                    )}
                  />

                  <FormField
                    control={control}
                    name="location"
                    render={({ field }) => (
                      <FormItem>
                        <FormLabel className="text-white">
                          Location / Sala Ricevimenti
                        </FormLabel>
                        <FormControl>
                          <Input
                            {...field}
                            placeholder="Es. Villa dei Tigli, Adrano"
                            className={cn(fieldClasses(!!errors.location), "h-11")}
                          />
                        </FormControl>
                        <FormMessage />
                      </FormItem>
                    )}
                  />

                  <FormField
                    control={control}
                    name="guests"
                    render={({ field }) => (
                      <FormItem>
                        <FormLabel className="text-white">
                          Numero stimato di ospiti
                        </FormLabel>
                        <FormControl>
                          <Input
                            type="number"
                            inputMode="numeric"
                            min={1}
                            placeholder="Es. 80"
                            {...field}
                            value={field.value ?? ""}
                            className={cn(
                              fieldClasses(!!errors.guests),
                              "h-11",
                            )}
                          />
                        </FormControl>
                        <FormMessage />
                      </FormItem>
                    )}
                  />

                  <FormField
                    control={control}
                    name="services"
                    render={({ field }) => (
                      <FormItem className="sm:col-span-2">
                        <FormLabel className="text-white">
                          Servizi desiderati
                        </FormLabel>
                        <FormDescription className="text-[#A1A1A1]">
                          Seleziona tutti quelli che ti interessano.{" "}
                          {watchedServices.length > 0 && (
                            <span className="text-[#D4AF37]">
                              {watchedServices.length} selezionat
                              {watchedServices.length === 1 ? "o" : "i"}
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </FormDescription>
                        <div className="gold-scroll mt-2 grid max-h-72 grid-cols-1 gap-2 overflow-y-auto pr-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
                          {serviceCheckboxes.map((item) => {
                            const checked = field.value?.includes(item.id);
                            return (
                              <div
                                key={item.id}
                                role="checkbox"
                                tabIndex={0}
                                aria-checked={!!checked}
                                aria-label={item.label}
                                onClick={() => {
                                  const next = checked
                                    ? field.value?.filter(
                                        (v: string) => v !== item.id,
                                      )
                                    : [...(field.value ?? []), item.id];
                                  field.onChange(next);
                                }}
                                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                                  if (e.key === " " || e.key === "Enter") {
                                    e.preventDefault();
                                    const next = checked
                                      ? field.value?.filter(
                                          (v: string) => v !== item.id,
                                        )
                                      : [...(field.value ?? []), item.id];
                                    field.onChange(next);
                                  }
                                }}
                                className={cn(
                                  "flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg border px-3 py-2.5 text-left text-sm transition-colors outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#D4AF37]/60",
                                  checked
                                    ? "border-[#D4AF37]/60 bg-[#D4AF37]/10 text-white"
                                    : "border-white/10 bg-[#0F0F0F]/60 text-[#A1A1A1] hover:border-[#D4AF37]/40 hover:text-white",
                                )}
                              >
                                {checked ? (
                                  <CheckSquare
                                    className="size-5 shrink-0 text-[#D4AF37]"
                                    strokeWidth={2}
                                  />
                                ) : (
                                  <Square
                                    className="size-5 shrink-0 text-white/30"
                                    strokeWidth={2}
                                  />
                                )}
                                <span className="leading-tight">
                                  {item.label}
                                </span>
                              </div>
                            );
                          })}
                        </div>
                        <FormMessage />
                      </FormItem>
                    )}
                  />

                  <FormField
                    control={control}
                    name="notes"
                    render={({ field }) => (
                      <FormItem className="sm:col-span-2">
                        <FormLabel className="text-white">
                          Note / Richieste Particolari{" "}
                          <span className="text-[#A1A1A1]">(opzionale)</span>
                        </FormLabel>
                        <FormControl>
                          <Textarea
                            {...field}
                            placeholder="Raccontami la tua idea, lo stile, eventuali fornitrici già ingaggiate…"
                            className={cn(
                              fieldClasses(!!errors.notes),
                              "min-h-28",
                            )}
                          />
                        </FormControl>
                        <FormMessage />
                      </FormItem>
                    )}
                  />

                  {/* Honeypot field — hidden from humans, bots fill it. aria-hidden + visually hidden. */}
                  <div
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    style={{
                      position: "absolute",
                      left: "-9999px",
                      top: "auto",
                      width: 1,
                      height: 1,
                      overflow: "hidden",
                    }}
                  >
                    <label htmlFor="website_hp">Website (leave empty)</label>
                    <input
                      id="website_hp"
                      type="text"
                      name="website"
                      tabIndex={-1}
                      autoComplete="off"
                      value={honeypotWebsite}
                      onChange={(e) => setHoneypotWebsite(e.target.value)}
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="sm:col-span-2 mt-2 flex flex-col items-stretch gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                    <p className="text-xs text-[#A1A1A1]">
                      Risposta garantita entro 24 ore · Nessun impegno.
                    </p>
                    <Button
                      type="submit"
                      disabled={isSubmitting}
                      className="h-12 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] px-7 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#0F0F0F] transition-all hover:bg-[#E5C75A] disabled:opacity-70"
                    >
                      {isSubmitting ? (
                        <>
                          <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin" />
                          Invio in corso…
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <Send className="size-4" strokeWidth={2} />
                          Invia richiesta
                        </>
                      )}
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </Form>
            )}
          </div>
        </Reveal>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function fieldClasses(hasError: boolean) {
  return cn(
    "rounded-lg border bg-[#0F0F0F]/60 text-white placeholder:text-[#6B6B6B]",
    "transition-colors focus-visible:ring-0",
    hasError
      ? "border-[#E5484D]/70 focus-visible:border-[#E5484D]"
      : "border-white/12 focus-visible:border-[#D4AF37]",
  );
}

function buildMessage(values: FormValues): string {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  lines.push("*RICHIESTA PREVENTIVO — Gold Events by Salvo*");
  lines.push("");
  lines.push(`👤 *Nome:* ${values.fullName}`);
  if (values.email) lines.push(`✉️ *Email:* ${values.email}`);
  if (values.phone) lines.push(`📞 *Telefono:* ${values.phone}`);
  lines.push(`🎉 *Tipo di evento:* ${values.eventType}`);
  lines.push(`📅 *Data:* ${values.eventDate}`);
  lines.push(`📍 *Location:* ${values.location}`);
  lines.push(`👥 *Ospiti stimati:* ${values.guests}`);
  if (values.services && values.services.length > 0) {
    const labels = values.services.map((id) => {
      const match = serviceCheckboxes.find((s) => s.id === id);
      return match ? match.label : id;
    });
    lines.push(`✨ *Servizi desiderati:*`);
    labels.forEach((l) => lines.push(`   • ${l}`));
  }
  if (values.notes && values.notes.trim().length > 0) {
    lines.push(`📝 *Note:* ${values.notes}`);
  }
  lines.push("");
  lines.push("— Inviato dal sito goldeventsbysalvo.it");
  return lines.join("\n");
}

function SuccessState({
  values,
  onReset,
}: {
  values: FormValues | null;
  onReset: () => void;
}) {
  // Build pre-filled links once
  const message = values ? buildMessage(values) : "";
  const encodedMessage = encodeURIComponent(message);

  // WhatsApp deep link: opens chat with Salvo, message pre-filled
  const whatsappUrl = `${contactInfo.whatsappHref}?text=${encodedMessage}`;

  // Email mailto: link with subject + body pre-filled
  const subject = `Richiesta preventivo — ${values?.eventType ?? "Evento"}`;
  const emailUrl = `mailto:${contactInfo.email}?subject=${encodeURIComponent(
    subject,
  )}&body=${encodedMessage}`;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-5 py-8 text-center sm:py-12">
      <span className="inline-flex size-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] text-[#0F0F0F]">
        <Check className="size-8" strokeWidth={2.5} />
      </span>
      <h3 className="font-serif text-2xl font-semibold text-white sm:text-3xl">
        Richiesta pronta per l'invio!
      </h3>
      <p className="max-w-md text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#A1A1A1] sm:text-base">
        Scegli come inviarla a <span className="font-semibold text-white">Salvo Piemonte</span>:
        riceverai risposta entro 24 ore con una proposta personalizzata.
      </p>

      {/* Two CTAs: WhatsApp + Email — both pre-filled */}
      <div className="mt-2 flex w-full max-w-md flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
        <a
          href={whatsappUrl}
          target="_blank"
          rel="noopener noreferrer"
          className="inline-flex h-12 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] px-5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#0F0F0F] transition-colors hover:bg-[#E5C75A]"
        >
          <MessageCircle className="size-4" strokeWidth={2} />
          Invia via WhatsApp
        </a>
        <a
          href={emailUrl}
          className="inline-flex h-12 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/60 px-5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors hover:bg-[#D4AF37] hover:text-[#0F0F0F]"
        >
          <Mail className="size-4" strokeWidth={2} />
          Invia via Email
        </a>
      </div>

      {/* Preview of the message that will be sent */}
      {values && (
        <details className="mt-4 w-full max-w-md text-left">
          <summary className="cursor-pointer text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#D4AF37]/80 hover:text-[#D4AF37]">
            Anteprima messaggio
          </summary>
          <pre className="mt-3 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-xl border border-white/8 bg-[#0F0F0F]/80 p-4 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-white/80 sm:text-xs">
            {message}
          </pre>
        </details>
      )}

      <Button
        type="button"
        onClick={onReset}
        variant="outline"
        className="mt-2 h-11 rounded-full border-[#D4AF37]/40 text-[#A1A1A1] hover:bg-transparent hover:text-white"
      >
        Invia un'altra richiesta
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { Camera, BadgePercent, ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";

import { SectionHeading } from "@/components/site/section-heading";
import { StaggerGroup, itemVariants } from "@/components/site/reveal";
import { Icon } from "@/components/site/icon";
import { extras } from "@/lib/site-content";
import { useDict } from "@/lib/i18n/use-lang";

export function Extras() {
  const t = useDict();
  return (
    <section
      id="extra"
      aria-labelledby="extras-title"
      className="relative scroll-mt-24 border-t border-white/5 bg-[#0F0F0F] py-20 sm:py-28"
    >
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <SectionHeading eyebrow={t.eyebrow.extras} title={t.extrasTitle} />

        {/* Highlight banner */}
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          viewport={{ once: true, amount: 0.4 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
          className="mx-auto mt-8 flex max-w-3xl items-center gap-4 rounded-2xl border border-[#D4AF37]/30 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#D4AF37]/10 to-transparent p-4 sm:p-5"
        >
          <span className="inline-flex size-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/40 bg-[#D4AF37]/15 text-[#D4AF37]">
            <Camera className="size-6" strokeWidth={1.5} />
          </span>
          <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-white sm:text-base">
            <span className="font-semibold text-[#D4AF37]">
              {t.extrasHighlightPrefix}
            </span>{" "}
            {t.extrasHighlightSuffix}{" "}
            <span className="font-semibold text-white">
              {t.extrasHighlightEmphasis}
            </span>.
          </p>
        </motion.div>

        <StaggerGroup
          className="mt-12 grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 sm:gap-5 lg:grid-cols-3"
          stagger={0.06}
        >
          {extras.map((extra) => (
            <ExtraCard
              key={extra.id}
              extra={extra}
              badgeLabel={t.extrasBadgeLabel}
              specsLabel={t.extrasSpecsLabel}
            />
          ))}
        </StaggerGroup>

        <p className="mt-8 text-center text-xs text-[#A1A1A1]">
          {t.extrasFooterNote}
        </p>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function ExtraCard({
  extra,
  badgeLabel,
  specsLabel,
}: {
  extra: (typeof extras)[number];
  badgeLabel: string;
  specsLabel: string;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);
  return (
    <motion.article
      variants={itemVariants}
      className="group relative flex h-full flex-col gap-3 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-white/8 bg-[#1C1C1C]/70 p-5 transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-[#D4AF37]/40 hover:shadow-[0_18px_50px_-20px_rgba(212,175,55,0.4)] sm:p-6"
    >
      <span
        aria-hidden
        className="pointer-events-none absolute -top-10 -right-10 size-24 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37]/5 blur-2xl transition-opacity duration-300 group-hover:bg-[#D4AF37]/15"
      />

      {/* Optional preview image — eager load for visibility */}
      {extra.image && (
        <div className="relative -mx-5 -mt-5 mb-1 h-32 overflow-hidden sm:-mx-6 sm:-mt-6 sm:h-36">
          {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
          <img
            src={extra.image}
            alt={extra.name}
            loading="eager"
            className="h-full w-full object-cover opacity-80 transition-all duration-500 group-hover:scale-105 group-hover:opacity-100"
          />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-[#1C1C1C] via-[#1C1C1C]/40 to-transparent" />
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
        <span className="inline-flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/40 bg-[#D4AF37]/10 text-[#D4AF37] transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105">
          <Icon name={extra.icon} className="size-5" />
        </span>
        <span
          title={badgeLabel}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/30 bg-[#D4AF37]/5 px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-[#D4AF37]"
        >
          <BadgePercent className="size-3" />
          {badgeLabel}
        </span>
      </div>

      <h3 className="font-serif text-base font-semibold leading-snug text-white sm:text-lg">
        {extra.name}
      </h3>
      <p className="flex-1 text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-[#A1A1A1] sm:text-sm">
        {extra.description}
      </p>

      <div className="mt-1 flex items-baseline gap-2">
        {extra.showPrice === false ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
            <span className="font-serif text-base font-semibold text-gold-gradient sm:text-lg">
              Prezzo su richiesta
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-[#6B6B6B]">
              Preventivo personalizzato in base ai servizi
            </span>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <span className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-3xl">
              {extra.priceLabel}
            </span>
            {extra.strikeThrough && (
              <span className="text-xs text-[#A1A1A1] line-through decoration-[#D4AF37]/50">
                {extra.strikeThrough}
              </span>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Expandable specs */}
      {extra.specs && extra.specs.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-2 border-t border-white/8 pt-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
            aria-expanded={open}
            className="flex w-full items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#D4AF37]/80 transition-colors hover:text-[#D4AF37]"
          >
            {specsLabel}
            <ChevronDown
              className={`size-3.5 transition-transform duration-300 ${open ? "rotate-180" : ""}`}
              strokeWidth={2}
            />
          </button>
          {open && (
            <dl className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2">
              {extra.specs.map((s, idx) => (
                <div key={idx} className="flex flex-col">
                  <dt className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-white/55">
                    {s.label}
                  </dt>
                  <dd className="text-xs leading-snug text-white/85 sm:text-[13px]">
                    {s.value}
                  </dd>
                </div>
              ))}
            </dl>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </motion.article>
  );
}
```

```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Sparkles, Mail, MessageCircle, MapPin, Instagram, User } from "lucide-react";

import { contactInfo, navItems as defaultNavItems } from "@/lib/site-content";
import { useDict } from "@/lib/i18n/use-lang";
import { useContentOverrides } from "@/lib/use-content-overrides";

function scrollTo(href: string) {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  const el = document.querySelector(href);
  if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
}

export function Footer() {
  const year = new Date().getFullYear();
  const t = useDict();
  const { overrides } = useContentOverrides();

  // Apply AI overrides to contact info (admin can change phone, email, instagram)
  const contact = {
    personName: overrides["contact.personName"] || contactInfo.personName,
    brandRole: overrides["contact.brandRole"] || contactInfo.brandRole,
    whatsapp: overrides["contact.whatsapp"] || contactInfo.whatsapp,
    whatsappHref: overrides["contact.whatsappHref"] ||
      `https://wa.me/${(overrides["contact.whatsapp"] || contactInfo.whatsapp).replace(/[^\d]/g, "")}`,
    email: overrides["contact.email"] || contactInfo.email,
    location: overrides["contact.location"] || contactInfo.location,
    instagram: overrides["contact.instagram"] || contactInfo.instagram,
    instagramHandle: overrides["contact.instagramHandle"] || contactInfo.instagramHandle,
  };

  // Map our 6 nav items to translated labels.
  const navItems = [
    { href: "#chi-sono", label: t.nav["chi-sono"] },
    { href: "#servizi", label: t.nav.servizi },
    { href: "#bundles", label: t.nav.bundle },
    { href: "#extra", label: t.nav.extra },
    { href: "#sicily-experience", label: t.nav["sicily-experience"] },
    { href: "#contatti", label: t.nav.contatti },
  ];

  return (
    <footer
      className="mt-auto border-t border-[#D4AF37]/15 bg-[#0A0A0A]"
      aria-labelledby="footer-heading"
    >
      <h2 id="footer-heading" className="sr-only">
        {t.footerSrTitle}
      </h2>

      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-14 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="grid gap-10 md:grid-cols-12">
          {/* Brand block */}
          <div className="md:col-span-5">
            <Link
              href="#top"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
              }}
              className="flex items-center gap-3"
              aria-label="Gold Events by Salvo — torna su"
            >
              <span className="flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/50 text-[#D4AF37]">
                <Sparkles className="size-4" strokeWidth={1.6} />
              </span>
              <span className="flex flex-col leading-none">
                <span className="font-serif text-base font-semibold tracking-wide text-white sm:text-lg">
                  GOLD EVENTS
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.34em] text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-[11px]">
                  by Salvo
                </span>
              </span>
            </Link>
            <p className="mt-5 max-w-sm text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#A1A1A1]">
              {t.footerTagline}
            </p>
            <p className="mt-5 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-white/90">
              <User className="size-3.5 text-[#D4AF37]" />
              <span className="font-semibold">{contact.personName}</span>
              <span className="text-[#A1A1A1]">· {contact.brandRole}</span>
            </p>
            <p className="mt-3 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-[#A1A1A1]">
              <MapPin className="size-3.5 text-[#D4AF37]" />
              {contact.location}
            </p>

            {/* Brand split note */}
            <p className="mt-6 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#6B6B6B]">
              <span className="text-[#D4AF37]">Gold Events by Salvo</span>{" "}
              ·{" "}
              <span className="text-[#A1A1A1]">
                Gold Events Sicily Experience
              </span>
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Quick links */}
          <nav
            aria-label={t.footerNavLabel}
            className="md:col-span-3"
          >
            <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.24em] text-[#D4AF37]">
              {t.footerNavLabel}
            </p>
            <ul className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 gap-y-2.5 sm:grid-cols-1">
              {navItems.map((item) => (
                <li key={item.href}>
                  <Link
                    href={item.href}
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.preventDefault();
                      scrollTo(item.href);
                    }}
                    className="text-sm text-[#A1A1A1] transition-colors hover:text-white"
                  >
                    {item.label}
                  </Link>
                </li>
              ))}
              <li>
                <Link
                  href="#partner"
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    scrollTo("#partner");
                  }}
                  className="text-sm text-[#A1A1A1] transition-colors hover:text-white"
                >
                  {t.footerPartnerLink}
                </Link>
              </li>
            </ul>
          </nav>

          {/* Contact CTAs */}
          <div className="md:col-span-4">
            <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.24em] text-[#D4AF37]">
              {t.footerContactsLabel}
            </p>
            <div className="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-3">
              <a
                href={contact.whatsappHref}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="group inline-flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/30 bg-[#1C1C1C]/60 px-4 py-3 transition-colors hover:border-[#D4AF37]/60 hover:bg-[#1C1C1C]"
              >
                <span className="inline-flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37]/10 text-[#D4AF37]">
                  <MessageCircle className="size-4" />
                </span>
                <span className="flex flex-col">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#A1A1A1]">
                    {t.footerWhatsapp}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-sm text-white">
                    {contact.whatsapp}
                  </span>
                </span>
              </a>
              <a
                href={`mailto:${contact.email}`}
                className="group inline-flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/30 bg-[#1C1C1C]/60 px-4 py-3 transition-colors hover:border-[#D4AF37]/60 hover:bg-[#1C1C1C]"
              >
                <span className="inline-flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37]/10 text-[#D4AF37]">
                  <Mail className="size-4" />
                </span>
                <span className="flex flex-col">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#A1A1A1]">
                    {t.footerEmail}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-sm text-white">
                    {contact.email}
                  </span>
                </span>
              </a>
            </div>

            {/* Social icons — only Instagram (other socials removed by user request) */}
            <div className="mt-6 flex items-center gap-3">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#A1A1A1]">
                {t.footerFollow}
              </span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <a
                  href={contact.instagram}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  aria-label={`Instagram ${contact.instagramHandle} — apri il profilo`}
                  title={`Seguimi su Instagram: ${contact.instagramHandle}`}
                  className="inline-flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-white/10 text-white/70 transition-colors hover:border-[#D4AF37]/50 hover:text-[#D4AF37]"
                >
                  <Instagram className="size-4" />
                </a>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-12 flex flex-col items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-white/8 pt-6 sm:flex-row">
          <p className="text-xs text-[#6B6B6B]">
            © {year} {t.footerCopyright}
          </p>
          <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#6B6B6B]">
            {t.footerRegion}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </footer>
  );
}
```
