```typescript
"use client";

import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { Sparkles, ArrowRight, Calendar } from "lucide-react";
import { useDict } from "@/lib/i18n/use-lang";

function scrollTo(href: string) {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  document.querySelector(href)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
}

export function GoldEventsHero() {
  const t = useDict();

  return (
    <section
      id="top"
      className="relative isolate overflow-hidden bg-[#0F0F0F]"
      aria-labelledby="ge-hero-title"
    >
      {/* Background image — contained, no overflow */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 -z-10 overflow-hidden" aria-hidden>
        <motion.div
          initial={{ scale: 1.05, opacity: 0 }}
          animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}
          transition={{ duration: 1.8, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
          className="absolute inset-0"
        >
          {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
          <img
            src="/images/collage/matrimoni-couple-toast.jpg"
            alt=""
            aria-hidden
            loading="eager"
            fetchPriority="high"
            className="h-full w-full object-cover opacity-40"
          />
          <div
            className="absolute inset-0"
            style={{
              background:
                "linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,15,15,0.70) 0%, rgba(15,15,15,0.88) 60%, #0F0F0F 100%)",
            }}
          />
          <div

            style={{
              background:
                "radial-gradient(ellipse 80% 60% at 50% 0%, rgba(212,175,55,0.18), transparent 60%)",
            }}
          />
        </motion.div>
      </div>

      <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[80svh] max-w-6xl flex-col justify-center px-4 pb-12 pt-20 sm:px-6 sm:pt-24 lg:px-8 lg:pt-28">
        {/* Eyebrow */}
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -12 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ delay: 0.2, duration: 0.7 }}

          {t.hero.titleLine1}{" "}
```

FILE: src/components/site/header.tsx
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Menu, X, Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Sheet,
  SheetContent,
  SheetHeader,
  SheetTitle,
  SheetTrigger,
  SheetClose,
} from "@/components/ui/sheet";
import { useDict } from "@/lib/i18n/use-lang";
import { LanguageSwitcher } from "@/components/site/language-switcher";

function scrollTo(href: string) {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  const el = document.querySelector(href);
  if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
}

function BrandMark({ compact = false }: { compact?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <Link
      href="/"
      className="group flex items-center gap-3"
      aria-label="Gold Events by Salvo — torna al bivio"
    >
      <span
        className={cn(
          "flex items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/50 bg-[#0F0F0F] text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors group-hover:border-[#D4AF37]",
          compact ? "size-9" : "size-10",
        )}
      >
        <Sparkles className="size-4" strokeWidth={1.6} aria-hidden />
      </span>
      <span className="flex flex-col leading-none">
        <span className="font-serif text-base font-semibold tracking-wide text-white sm:text-lg">
          GOLD EVENTS
        </span>
        <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.34em] text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-[11px]">
          by Salvo
        </span>
      </span>
    </Link>
  );
}

export function Header() {
  const [scrolled, setScrolled] = React.useState(false);
  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const t = useDict();

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const onScroll = () => setScrolled(window.scrollY > 24);
    onScroll();
    window.addEventListener("scroll", onScroll, { passive: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener("scroll", onScroll);
  }, []);

  // Context-aware navigation: detect current page
  const [pathname, setPathname] = React.useState("");
  React.useEffect(() => {
    setPathname(window.location.pathname);
  }, []);

  // On /gold-events: show sections of that page + link to /sicily-experience
  // On /sicily-experience: show sections of that page + link to /gold-events
  // On / (bivio): link to both subpages
  const navItems = React.useMemo(() => {
    if (pathname === "/gold-events") {
      return [
        { href: "#chi-sono", label: "Chi Sono", type: "anchor" },
        { href: "#servizi", label: "Servizi", type: "anchor" },
        { href: "#bundles", label: "Bundle", type: "anchor" },
        { href: "#extra", label: "Extra", type: "anchor" },
        { href: "#contatti", label: "Contatti", type: "anchor" },
        { href: "/eventi", label: "Eventi", type: "link" },
        { href: "/sicily-experience", label: "Sicily Experience", type: "link" },
      ];
    } else if (pathname === "/sicily-experience") {
      return [
        { href: "#sicily-experience", label: "Esperienze", type: "anchor" },
        { href: "#contatti", label: "Contatti", type: "anchor" },
        { href: "#partner", label: "Partner", type: "anchor" },
        { href: "/eventi", label: "Eventi", type: "link" },
        { href: "/gold-events", label: "Gold Events", type: "link" },
      ];
    } else {
      // Bivio / home / altre pagine
      return [
        { href: "/gold-events", label: "Gold Events", type: "link" },
        { href: "/eventi", label: "Eventi", type: "link" },
        { href: "/gold-eventi", label: "Gold Eventi", type: "link" },
        { href: "/salvo-piemonte", label: "Salvo Piemonte", type: "link" },
        { href: "/prezzi", label: "Prezzi", type: "link" },
        { href: "/dove-opero", label: "Dove Opero", type: "link" },
        { href: "/faq", label: "FAQ", type: "link" },
        { href: "/sicily-experience", label: "Sicily Experience", type: "link" },
      ];
    }
  }, [pathname]);

  return (
    <header
      className={cn(
        "sticky top-0 z-50 w-full transition-all duration-300",
        scrolled
          ? "border-b border-[#D4AF37]/15 bg-[#0F0F0F]/90 backdrop-blur-xl"
          : "border-b border-transparent bg-[#0F0F0F]/40 backdrop-blur-md",
      )}
    >
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-7xl items-center justify-between px-4 sm:h-20 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <BrandMark compact />

        {/* Desktop nav */}
        <nav
          aria-label="Navigazione principale"
          className="hidden items-center gap-1 lg:flex"
        >
          {navItems.map((item) => (
            <Link
              key={item.href}
              href={item.href}
              onClick={(e) => {
                if (item.type === "anchor") {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  scrollTo(item.href);
                }
                // If type is "link", let Next.js handle the navigation
              }}
              className="relative rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-[#A1A1A1] transition-colors hover:text-white"
            >
              {item.label}
            </Link>
          ))}
        </nav>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {/* Language switcher (desktop) */}
          <LanguageSwitcher variant="header" className="hidden sm:inline-flex" />

          <Button
            asChild
            className="hidden h-10 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] px-5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#0F0F0F] shadow-[0_8px_24px_-8px_rgba(212,175,55,0.55)] transition-all hover:bg-[#E5C75A] hover:shadow-[0_10px_30px_-8px_rgba(212,175,55,0.7)] sm:inline-flex"
          >
            <a
              href="#contatti"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                scrollTo("#contatti");
              }}
            >
              {t.nav.richiediDisponibilita}
            </a>
          </Button>

          {/* Mobile hamburger */}
          <Sheet open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
            <SheetTrigger asChild>
              <button
                className="inline-flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/30 text-white transition-colors hover:border-[#D4AF37] hover:text-[#D4AF37] lg:hidden"
                aria-label={t.openMenu}
              >
                <Menu className="size-5" />
              </button>
            </SheetTrigger>
            <SheetContent
              side="right"
              className="gold-scroll w-[88%] max-w-sm overflow-y-auto border-l border-[#D4AF37]/20 bg-[#0F0F0F] p-0"
            >
              <SheetHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-white/5 px-6 py-5">
                <SheetTitle className="text-left">
                  <span className="block font-serif text-base font-semibold tracking-wide text-white">
                    GOLD EVENTS
                  </span>
                  <span className="block text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.34em] text-[#D4AF37]">
                    by Salvo
                  </span>
                </SheetTitle>
                <SheetClose
                  aria-label={t.closeMenu}
                  className="rounded-full border border-white/10 p-2 text-white/70 transition-colors hover:border-[#D4AF37]/50 hover:text-white"
                >
                  <X className="size-4" />
                </SheetClose>
              </SheetHeader>

              <nav
                aria-label="Navigazione mobile"
                className="flex flex-col px-4 py-4"
              >
                {navItems.map((item, idx) => (
                  <motion.div
                    key={item.href}
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 12 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                    transition={{ delay: 0.04 * idx + 0.05 }}
                  >
                    <Link
                      href={item.href}
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        if (item.type === "anchor") {
                          e.preventDefault();
                          setOpen(false);
                          setTimeout(() => scrollTo(item.href), 80);
                        } else {
                          setOpen(false);
                        }
                      }}
                      className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl px-4 py-4 text-base font-medium text-white transition-colors hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-[#D4AF37]"
                    >
                      {item.label}
                      <span className="text-[#D4AF37]/40">→</span>
                    </Link>
                  </motion.div>
                ))}
              </nav>

              <div className="mt-auto flex flex-col gap-3 px-6 pb-8">
                <LanguageSwitcher variant="sheet" />
                <Button
                  asChild
                  className="h-12 w-full rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#0F0F0F]"
                >
                  <a
                    href="#contatti"
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.preventDefault();
                      setOpen(false);
                      setTimeout(() => scrollTo("#contatti"), 80);
                    }}
                  >
                    {t.nav.richiediDisponibilita}
                  </a>
                </Button>
                <p className="text-center text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.24em] text-[#A1A1A1]">
                  {t.mobileNavLocation}
                </p>
              </div>
            </SheetContent>
          </Sheet>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { ArrowRight, ChevronDown, Sparkles, MapPin } from "lucide-react";

function scrollTo(href: string) {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  const el = document.querySelector(href);
  if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
}

interface BrandCardData {
  variant: "local" | "international";
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  tag: string;
  badge?: string;
  href: string;
  cta: string;
  /** Real photo path (collage panel) shown as cover image at top of card */
  image?: string;
  /** Short caption displayed as pill on the photo */
  imageCaption?: string;
}

export function HeroSplit() {
  const t = useDict();

  const brandCards: BrandCardData[] = [
    {
      variant: "local",
      icon: <Sparkles className="size-6" strokeWidth={1.5} />,
      title: t.hero.cardLocalTitle,
      subtitle: t.hero.cardLocalSubtitle,
      tag: t.hero.cardLocalTag,
      href: "#servizi",
      cta: t.hero.cardLocalCta,
      image: "/images/collage/matrimoni-couple-toast.jpg",
      imageCaption: "Matrimoni · Feste · Eventi",
    },
    {
      variant: "international",
      icon: <MapPin className="size-6" strokeWidth={1.5} />,
      title: t.hero.cardIntlTitle,
      subtitle: t.hero.cardIntlSubtitle,
      tag: t.hero.cardIntlTag,
      badge: t.hero.cardIntlBadge,
      href: "#sicily-experience",
      image: "/images/collage/sicily-aerial-etna-taormina.jpg",
      imageCaption: "Etna · Taormina · Sicilia",
      cta: t.hero.cardIntlCta,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <section
      id="top"
      aria-labelledby="hero-title"
      className="relative isolate overflow-hidden bg-[#0F0F0F]"
    >
      {/* Background layers */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 -z-10" aria-hidden>
        {/* Generated Etna-at-dusk hero image as base */}
        <motion.div
          initial={{ scale: 1.08, opacity: 0 }}
          animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}
          transition={{ duration: 1.8, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
          className="absolute inset-0"
        >
          { }
          <img
            src="/images/hero-etna-dusk.png"
            alt=""
            aria-hidden
            className="h-full w-full object-cover opacity-55"
          />
          {/* Dark overlay for legibility */}
          <div
            className="absolute inset-0"
            style={{
              background:
                "linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,15,15,0.55) 0%, rgba(15,15,15,0.75) 50%, #0F0F0F 100%)",
            }}
          />
          {/* Gold radial accent */}
          <div
            className="absolute inset-0"
            style={{
              background:
                "radial-gradient(ellipse 80% 60% at 50% 0%, rgba(212,175,55,0.18), transparent 60%)",
            }}
          />
        </motion.div>
        {/* Geometric dotted grid */}
        <div className="hero-grid absolute inset-0 opacity-40" />
      </div>

      <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[100svh] max-w-7xl flex-col justify-center px-4 pb-16 pt-24 sm:px-6 sm:pt-28 lg:px-8 lg:pt-32">
        {/* Eyebrow */}
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 16 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.7, ease: "easeOut" }}
          className="flex justify-center"
        >
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/30 bg-[#D4AF37]/5 px-4 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.34em] text-[#D4AF37] sm:text-[11px]">
            <MapPin className="size-3" />
            {t.hero.locationBadge}
          </span>
        </motion.div>

        {/* H1 */}
        <motion.h1
          id="hero-title"
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 24 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.9, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1], delay: 0.08 }}
          className="mx-auto mt-6 max-w-5xl text-balance text-center font-serif text-[2.6rem] leading-[1.04] font-semibold text-white sm:text-6xl lg:text-7xl"
        >
          {t.hero.titleLine1}
          <span className="block text-gold-gradient">
            {t.hero.titleLine2}
          </span>
        </motion.h1>

        {/* Subtitle */}
        <motion.p
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.9, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1], delay: 0.18 }}
          className="mx-auto mt-6 max-w-2xl text-center text-base leading-relaxed text-[#A1A1A1] sm:text-lg"
        >
          {t.hero.subtitle}
        </motion.p>

        {/* CTAs */}
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.9, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1], delay: 0.28 }}
          className="mt-9 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 sm:flex-row"
        >
          <Button
            asChild
            className="h-12 w-full rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] px-7 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#0F0F0F] shadow-[0_14px_40px_-10px_rgba(212,175,55,0.65)] transition-all hover:bg-[#E5C75A] hover:shadow-[0_18px_50px_-10px_rgba(212,175,55,0.8)] sm:w-auto"
          >
            <a
              href="#contatti"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                scrollTo("#contatti");
              }}
            >
              {t.hero.primaryCta}
            </a>
          </Button>
          <Button
            asChild
            variant="outline"
            className="h-12 w-full rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/60 bg-transparent px-7 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#D4AF37] transition-all hover:bg-[#D4AF37] hover:text-[#0F0F0F] sm:w-auto"
          >
            <a
              href="#servizi"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                scrollTo("#servizi");
              }}
            >
              {t.hero.secondaryCta}
            </a>
          </Button>
        </motion.div>

        {/* Brand split */}
        <div className="mx-auto mt-16 w-full max-w-6xl">
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.8, delay: 0.45 }}
            className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-center gap-3"
          >
            <span className="h-px w-10 bg-[#D4AF37]/40" />
            <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.32em] text-[#D4AF37]/90">
              {t.hero.brandSplitLabel}
            </span>
            <span className="h-px w-10 bg-[#D4AF37]/40" />
          </motion.div>

          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:gap-5 md:grid-cols-2 md:items-stretch">
            {brandCards.map((card, idx) => (
              <motion.div
                key={card.title}
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 24 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                transition={{
                  duration: 0.7,
                  delay: 0.55 + idx * 0.12,
                  ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1],
                }}
              >
                <BrandCard card={card} />
              </motion.div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Scroll hint */}
      <motion.button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => scrollTo("#chi-sono")}
        aria-label={t.hero.scrollHint}
        initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
        transition={{ delay: 1.2, duration: 0.6 }}
        className="absolute bottom-5 left-1/2 hidden -translate-x-1/2 flex-col items-center gap-1 text-[#D4AF37]/70 transition-colors hover:text-[#D4AF37] sm:flex"
      >
        <span className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]">{t.hero.scrollHint}</span>
        <motion.span
          animate={{ y: [0, 6, 0] }}
          transition={{ duration: 1.6, repeat: Infinity, ease: "easeInOut" }}
        >
          <ChevronDown className="size-4" />
        </motion.span>
      </motion.button>
    </section>
  );
}

function BrandCard({ card }: { card: BrandCardData }) {
  return (
    <article
      className={cn(
        "group relative flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-0.5",
        card.variant === "international"
          ? "border-[#D4AF37]/30 bg-gradient-to-br from-[#1C1C1C] to-[#0F0F0F]"
          : "border-white/10 bg-[#1C1C1C]/80 hover:border-[#D4AF37]/40",
      )}
    >
      {/* Cover image (collage panel) — contained, no overflow, fills card width */}
      {card.image && (
        <div className="relative w-full overflow-hidden">
          <div className="aspect-[3/4] w-full">
            { }
            <img
              src={card.image}
              alt={card.title}
              className="h-full w-full object-cover opacity-90 transition-all duration-500 group-hover:scale-105 group-hover:opacity-100"
            />
          </div>
          <div
            aria-hidden
            className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-[#0F0F0F] via-[#0F0F0F]/35 to-transparent"
          />
          {card.imageCaption && (
            <span className="absolute bottom-2 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/40 bg-[#0F0F0F]/80 px-3 py-1 text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-[#D4AF37] backdrop-blur-sm">
              {card.imageCaption}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Card body */}
      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-4 p-6 sm:p-7">
        {/* Decorative corner */}
        <span
          aria-hidden
          className="pointer-events-none absolute -top-12 -right-12 size-32 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37]/10 blur-2xl transition-opacity group-hover:bg-[#D4AF37]/20"
        />

        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
          <span className="inline-flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/40 bg-[#D4AF37]/10 text-[#D4AF37]">
            {card.icon}
          </span>
          <span className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1.5">
            <span className="rounded-full border border-white/10 px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-white/80">
              {card.tag}
            </span>
            {card.badge && (
              <span className="rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#0F0F0F]">
                {card.badge}
              </span>
            )}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1">
          <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-semibold leading-tight text-white sm:text-2xl">
            {card.title}
          </h3>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#A1A1A1] sm:text-base">
            {card.subtitle}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => scrollTo(card.href)}
          className="group/cta mt-2 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors hover:text-[#E5C75A]"
        >
          {card.cta}
          <ArrowRight
            className="size-3.5 transition-transform group-hover/cta:translate-x-1"
            strokeWidth={2}
          />
        </button>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}
```

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: src/components/site/human-chat-widget.tsx
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { MessageCircle, X, Send, Loader2, User, Headphones, ArrowLeft } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface ChatMessage {
  id: string;
  role: "visitor" | "admin" | "system";
  text: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

interface Conversation {
  id: string;
  status: string;
  unreadByVisitor: number;
  messages: ChatMessage[];
  visitorName?: string | null;
}

interface HumanChatWidgetProps {
  /** Called when user wants to switch back to AI bot */
  onBackToBot?: () => void;
}

/**
 * Human Chat Widget — chat bidirezionale con Salvo
 *
 * - Cliente (visitatore) manda messaggi in tempo reale
 * - Salvo risponde dal mini gestionale admin
 * - Polling ogni 8 secondi per nuovi messaggi
 * - Cookie persistente per ritrovare la conversazione
 */
export function HumanChatWidget({ onBackToBot }: HumanChatWidgetProps) {
  const [conversation, setConversation] = React.useState<Conversation | null>(null);
  const [messages, setMessages] = React.useState<ChatMessage[]>([]);
  const [input, setInput] = React.useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(false);
  const [sending, setSending] = React.useState(false);
  const [showInfo, setShowInfo] = React.useState(false);

  const scrollRef = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const inputRef = React.useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const pollIntervalRef = React.useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);

  // Carica conversazione esistente all'avvio
  const loadConversation = React.useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/chat/conversations", { cache: "no-store" });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.conversation) {
        setConversation(data.conversation);
        setMessages(data.conversation.messages ?? data.messages ?? []);
      }
    } catch {
      // Silenzioso — fallback
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    loadConversation();
  }, [loadConversation]);

  // Polling per nuovi messaggi (solo se conversation esiste)
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!conversation) return;
    const poll = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/chat/conversations", { cache: "no-store" });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.conversation) {
          setConversation(data.conversation);
          setMessages(data.conversation.messages ?? []);
        }
      } catch {}
    };
    pollIntervalRef.current = setInterval(poll, 8000); // 8 secondi
    return () => {
      if (pollIntervalRef.current) clearInterval(pollIntervalRef.current);
    };
  }, [conversation?.id]);

  // Scroll to bottom su nuovo messaggio
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (scrollRef.current) {
      scrollRef.current.scrollTop = scrollRef.current.scrollHeight;
    }
  }, [messages]);

  const startConversation = async (meta?: {
    name?: string;
    email?: string;
    phone?: string;
  }) => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/chat/conversations", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          visitorName: meta?.name,
          visitorEmail: meta?.email,
          visitorPhone: meta?.phone,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.conversation) {
        setConversation(data.conversation);
        setMessages(data.conversation.messages ?? []);
      }
    } catch {
      toast.error("Errore avvio chat. Riprova.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const sendMessage = async () => {
    const text = input.trim();
    if (!text || !conversation || sending) return;

    setInput("");
    setSending(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(
        `/api/chat/conversations/${conversation.id}/messages`,
        {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ text }),
        },
      );
      if (!res.ok) {
        const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        toast.error(err.error || "Errore invio messaggio");
        return;
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.message) {
        setMessages((m) => [...m, data.message]);
      }
      // Aggiorna conversazione (potrebbe essere cambiato lo status)
      loadConversation();
    } catch {
      toast.error("Errore connessione. Riprova.");
    } finally {
      setSending(false);
      inputRef.current?.focus();
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center">
        <Loader2 className="size-6 animate-spin text-[#D4AF37]" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!conversation) {
    return (
      <StartChatCard onStart={startConversation} onBackToBot={onBackToBot} />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-white/8 px-4 py-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <span className="relative inline-flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/40 bg-[#D4AF37]/10">
            <Headphones className="size-4 text-[#D4AF37]" />
            <span className="absolute -bottom-0.5 -right-0.5 size-3 rounded-full border-2 border-[#1C1C1C] bg-green-500" />
          </span>
          <div className="flex flex-col leading-tight">
            <p className="font-serif text-sm font-semibold text-white">
              Salvo Piemonte
            </p>
            <p className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] text-[#A1A1A1]">
              <span className="size-1.5 rounded-full bg-green-500" />
              Online · risponde entro 24h
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        {onBackToBot && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onBackToBot}
            className="inline-flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[#A1A1A1] transition-colors hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-white"
            aria-label="Torna all'assistente AI"
            title="Torna all'assistente AI"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="size-4" />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Messages */}
      <div
        ref={scrollRef}
        className="flex-1 space-y-2 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-3"
        style={{ scrollBehavior: "smooth" }}
      >
        {messages.length === 0 && (
          <p className="py-4 text-center text-xs text-[#A1A1A1]">
            Inizia la conversazione scrivendo qui sotto 👇
          </p>
        )}
        {messages.map((m) => (
          <MessageBubble key={m.id} message={m} />
        ))}
        {sending && (
          <div className="flex justify-start">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-2xl border border-white/8 bg-[#0F0F0F] px-3.5 py-2.5">
              {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
                <motion.span
                  key={i}
                  className="size-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37]"
                  animate={{ opacity: [0.3, 1, 0.3] }}
                  transition={{
                    duration: 1,
                    repeat: Infinity,
                    delay: i * 0.2,
                  }}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Info banner */}
      {conversation.status === "waiting" && (
        <div className="border-t border-[#D4AF37]/20 bg-[#D4AF37]/5 px-4 py-2">
          <p className="text-[10px] text-[#D4AF37]">
            ⏳ Messaggio inviato. Salvo risponderà appena possibile.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Input */}
      <div className="border-t border-white/8 p-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <input
            ref={inputRef}
            type="text"
            value={input}
            onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={(e) => {
              if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) {
                e.preventDefault();
                sendMessage();
              }
            }}
            placeholder="Scrivi un messaggio…"
            maxLength={2000}
            disabled={sending || conversation.status === "closed"}
            className="h-10 flex-1 rounded-full border border-white/12 bg-[#0F0F0F] px-4 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-[#6B6B6B] focus:border-[#D4AF37] focus:outline-none disabled:opacity-60"
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={sendMessage}
            disabled={!input.trim() || sending || conversation.status === "closed"}
            className="inline-flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] text-[#0F0F0F] transition-colors hover:bg-[#E5C75A] disabled:opacity-40"
            aria-label="Invia messaggio"
          >
            {sending ? (
              <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin" />
            ) : (
              <Send className="size-4" />
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-center text-[10px] text-[#6B6B6B]">
          {conversation.status === "closed"
            ? "Conversazione chiusa"
            : "Premi Invio per spedire · Auto-refresh ogni 8s"}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function MessageBubble({ message }: { message: ChatMessage }) {
  if (message.role === "system") {
    return (
      <div className="flex justify-center py-1">
        <span className="rounded-full border border-white/8 bg-[#1C1C1C]/80 px-3 py-1 text-[10px] italic text-[#A1A1A1]">
          {message.text}
        </span>
      </div>
    );
  }
  const isVisitor = message.role === "visitor";
  return (
    <div className={`flex ${isVisitor ? "justify-end" : "justify-start"}`}>
      <div
        className={cn(
          "max-w-[85%] rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm leading-relaxed",
          isVisitor
            ? "rounded-br-md bg-[#D4AF37] text-[#0F0F0F]"
            : "rounded-bl-md border border-white/8 bg-[#0F0F0F] text-white/90",
        )}
      >
        <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">{message.text}</p>
        <p
          className={cn(
            "mt-1 text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wide",
            isVisitor ? "text-[#0F0F0F]/60" : "text-[#6B6B6B]",
          )}
        >
          {new Date(message.createdAt).toLocaleTimeString("it-IT", {
            hour: "2-digit",
            minute: "2-digit",
          })}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function StartChatCard({
  onStart,
  onBackToBot,
}: {
  onStart: (meta?: { name?: string; email?: string; phone?: string }) => void;
  onBackToBot?: () => void;
}) {
  const [name, setName] = React.useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = React.useState("");
  const [phone, setPhone] = React.useState("");
  const [starting, setStarting] = React.useState(false);

  const handleStart = async () => {
    setStarting(true);
    await onStart({
      name: name.trim() || undefined,
      email: email.trim() || undefined,
      phone: phone.trim() || undefined,
    });
    setStarting(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-white/8 px-4 py-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <span className="inline-flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/40 bg-[#D4AF37]/10">
            <Headphones className="size-4 text-[#D4AF37]" />
          </span>
          <div>
            <p className="font-serif text-sm font-semibold text-white">
              Parla con Salvo
            </p>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-[#A1A1A1]">Chat diretta</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        {onBackToBot && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onBackToBot}
            className="inline-flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[#A1A1A1] transition-colors hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-white"
            aria-label="Torna all'assistente AI"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="size-4" />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-sm space-y-4">
          <div className="text-center">
            <div className="mx-auto mb-3 inline-flex size-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/40 bg-[#D4AF37]/10">
              <User className="size-7 text-[#D4AF37]" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="font-serif text-base font-semibold text-white">
              Ciao, sono Salvo
            </h3>
            <p className="mt-1.5 text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#A1A1A1]">
              Sono disponibile per rispondere a tutte le tue domande su matrimoni,
              eventi, prezzi e disponibilità. Rispondo entro 24 ore.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3 rounded-xl border border-white/8 bg-[#0F0F0F]/60 p-4">
            <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#A1A1A1]">
              Come ti chiami? (opzionale)
            </p>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Nome e cognome"
              className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/12 bg-[#1C1C1C] px-3 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-[#6B6B6B] focus:border-[#D4AF37] focus:outline-none"
            />
            <input
              type="email"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Email (opzionale)"
              className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/12 bg-[#1C1C1C] px-3 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-[#6B6B6B] focus:border-[#D4AF37] focus:outline-none"
            />
            <input
              type="tel"
              value={phone}
              onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Telefono (opzionale)"
              className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/12 bg-[#1C1C1C] px-3 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-[#6B6B6B] focus:border-[#D4AF37] focus:outline-none"
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleStart}
              disabled={starting}
              className="inline-flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] px-5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#0F0F0F] transition-colors hover:bg-[#E5C75A] disabled:opacity-60"
            >
              {starting ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin" />
                  Avvio…
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <MessageCircle className="size-4" />
                  Inizia la chat
                </>
              )}
            </button>
            <p className="text-center text-[10px] text-[#6B6B6B]">
              Puoi anche omettere i dati e iniziare subito
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```typescript
import {
  Baby,

  Heart,
  GraduationCap,
  Lock,
  Drama,
  Building2,
  Disc3,
  Dices,
  Camera,
  Video,
  Phone,
  Sparkles,
  Clock,
  Car,
  Wine,
  Sun,
  Tractor,
  Waves,
  Star,
  type LucideIcon,
} from "lucide-react";

import type { IconName } from "@/lib/site-content";

const iconMap: Record<IconName, LucideIcon> = {
  Baby,
  Cake,
  Heart,
  GraduationCap,
  Lock,
  Drama,
  Building2,
  Disc3,
  Dices,
  Camera,
  Video,
  Phone,
  Sparkles,
  Clock,
  Car,
  Wine,
  Sun,
  Tractor,
  Waves,
  Star,
};

interface IconProps {
  name: IconName;
  className?: string;
  strokeWidth?: number;
}

export function Icon({ name, className, strokeWidth = 1.5 }: IconProps) {
  const Cmp = iconMap[name];
  return <Cmp className={className} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} aria-hidden />;
}
```

FILE: src/components/site/language-switcher.tsx

```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { Check, Globe } from "lucide-react";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { LANG_META, LANGS, type Lang } from "@/lib/i18n/dictionaries";
import { useLang } from "@/lib/i18n/use-lang";

interface LanguageSwitcherProps {
  variant?: "header" | "sheet";
  className?: string;
}

/**
 * Simple language switcher with native dropdown (no Radix UI).
 * Works on Vercel serverless without issues.
 */
export function LanguageSwitcher({
  variant = "header",
  className,
}: LanguageSwitcherProps) {
  const lang = useLang((s) => s.lang);
  const setLang = useLang((s) => s.setLang);
  const meta = LANG_META[lang];
  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const ref = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Close on outside click
  React.useEffect(() => {
    function handler(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setOpen(false);
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handler);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handler);
  }, []);

  const triggerClass = cn(
    "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/30 bg-[#0F0F0F]/40 px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors hover:border-[#D4AF37] hover:bg-[#D4AF37]/10 cursor-pointer",
    variant === "sheet" && "w-full justify-center py-2.5",
    className,
  );

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="relative inline-block">
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-label={`Change language — current: ${meta.english}`}
        className={triggerClass}
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
      >
        <Globe className="size-3.5" strokeWidth={2} />
        <span>{meta.short}</span>
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div
          className="gold-scroll absolute right-0 top-full z-[100] mt-2 max-h-[70vh] w-56 overflow-y-auto rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/25 bg-[#0F0F0F] py-2 text-white shadow-[0_24px_60px_-20px_rgba(0,0,0,0.95)]"
          style={{ minWidth: "200px" }}
        >
          <div className="px-3 pb-2 pt-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.24em] text-[#D4AF37]">
            Lingua · Language
          </div>
          <div className="mx-3 mb-1 h-px bg-white/8" />
          {LANGS.map((l: Lang) => {
            const m = LANG_META[l];
            const active = l === lang;
            return (
              <button
                key={l}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setLang(l);
                  setOpen(false);
                }}
                className={cn(
                  "flex w-full cursor-pointer items-center justify-between gap-3 px-3 py-2 text-sm transition-colors",
                  active
                    ? "bg-[#D4AF37]/15 text-white"
                    : "text-white/85 hover:bg-[#D4AF37]/8 hover:text-white",
                )}
              >
                <span className="flex flex-col text-left">
                  <span className="font-medium leading-tight">{m.native}</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#A1A1A1]">
                    {m.english}
                  </span>
                </span>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#D4AF37]">
                    {m.short}
                  </span>
                  {active && <Check className="size-3.5 text-[#D4AF37]" strokeWidth={2.5} />}
                </span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

FILE: src/components/site/media-display.tsx
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { useMedia, type MediaItem } from "@/lib/use-media";

/**
 * Displays media (images + videos) added by Salvo from the admin panel.
 * Ultra HD quality, instant loading, super fluid.
 */
export function MediaDisplay({ placement }: { placement?: string }) {
  const { items, loading } = useMedia(placement);

  if (loading) return null;
  if (items.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <section className="relative py-12 sm:py-16" aria-labelledby="media-gallery-title">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="mb-8 text-center">
          <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.32em] text-[#D4AF37]">
            Galleria Gold Events
          </p>
          <h2 id="media-gallery-title" className="mt-2 font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-white sm:text-3xl">
            Momenti indimenticabili
          </h2>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[#A1A1A1]">Foto e video reali degli eventi di Salvo Piemonte</p>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {items.map((item, idx) => (
            <MediaCard key={item.id} item={item} index={idx} />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function MediaCard({ item, index }: { item: MediaItem; index: number }) {
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = React.useState(false);
  const isYouTube = item.url.includes("youtube.com") || item.url.includes("youtu.be");

  let youtubeId = "";
  if (isYouTube) {
    const match = item.url.match(/(?:youtube\.com\/watch\?v=|youtu\.be\/)([^&\s]+)/);
    if (match) youtubeId = match[1];
  }

  // Optimize image URL for ultra HD quality
  const optimizedUrl = item.type === "image" ? optimizeImageUrl(item.url) : item.url;

  return (
    <article
      className="group relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#D4AF37]/20 bg-[#1C1C1C]/70 transition-all duration-500 hover:border-[#D4AF37]/40 hover:shadow-[0_20px_60px_-20px_rgba(212,175,55,0.3)]"
      style={{ animationDelay: `${index * 80}ms` }}
    >
      {item.type === "video" && youtubeId ? (
        <div className="aspect-video w-full overflow-hidden bg-black">
          <iframe
            src={`https://www.youtube.com/embed/${youtubeId}?rel=0&modestbranding=1&hd=1`}
            title={item.title || "Video Gold Events"}
            allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture"
            allowFullScreen
            loading="lazy"
            className="h-full w-full"
          />
        </div>
      ) : item.type === "image" ? (
        <div className="relative aspect-[4/3] w-full overflow-hidden bg-[#0F0F0F]">
          {!loaded && (
            <div className="absolute inset-0 animate-pulse bg-[#1C1C1C]" />
          )}
          {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
          <img
            src={optimizedUrl}
            alt={item.title || "Gold Events by Salvo"}
            loading="lazy"
            fetchPriority={index < 3 ? "high" : "auto"}
            onLoad={() => setLoaded(true)}
            className={`h-full w-full object-cover transition-all duration-700 ${
              loaded ? "opacity-100 scale-100 group-hover:scale-110" : "opacity-0"
            }`}
          />
          {/* HD badge */}
          <span className="absolute right-3 top-3 inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-[#0F0F0F]/80 px-2.5 py-1 text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-[#D4AF37] backdrop-blur-sm">
            <span className="size-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37]" />
            HD
          </span>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {item.title && (
        <div className="p-4">
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-white">{item.title}</p>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-[#D4AF37]/60">
            {item.type === "video" ? "🎥 Video" : "📷 Foto"}
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
    </article>
  );
}

/**
 * Optimize image URL for ultra HD quality.
 * For Unsplash: add quality + width params
 * For Google Drive: convert to direct link
 * For Imgur: add quality suffix
 * For others: return as-is (already optimized by source)
 */
function optimizeImageUrl(url: string): string {
  // Unsplash: add ultra HD params
  if (url.includes("unsplash.com")) {
    const separator = url.includes("?") ? "&" : "?";
    return `${url}${separator}q=100&w=1600&auto=format&fit=crop`;
  }

  // Google Drive: convert share link to direct image
  if (url.includes("drive.google.com") && url.includes("file/d/")) {
    const match = url.match(/file\/d\/([^\/]+)/);
    if (match) return `https://drive.google.com/uc?export=view&id=${match[1]}`;
  }

  // Imgur: add high quality suffix
  if (url.includes("imgur.com") && url.endsWith(".jpg")) {
    return url.replace(".jpg", ".jpg");
  }

  return url;
}
```

```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { z } from "zod";
import { Loader2, Send, Check, ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { RadioGroup, RadioGroupItem } from "@/components/ui/radio-group";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import {
  Form,
  FormControl,
  FormDescription,
  FormField,
  FormItem,
  FormLabel,
  FormMessage,
} from "@/components/ui/form";
import { partnerActivityOptions } from "@/lib/site-content";

const formSchema = z.object({
  businessName: z
    .string()
    .min(2, "Inserisci la ragione sociale o il nome della struttura."),
  activityType: z.string({
    required_error: "Seleziona la tipologia di attività.",
  }),
  location: z.string().min(2, "Indica la sede operativa."),
  referentName: z
    .string()
    .min(2, "Inserisci nome e ruolo del referente."),
  referentPhone: z
    .string()
    .min(6, "Inserisci un numero di telefono valido."),
  referentEmail: z.string().email("Inserisci un'email valida."),
  capacity: z.string().optional(),
  hasPower: z.string({ required_error: "Indica la presenza di allaccio elettrico." }),
  b2bNotes: z.string().optional(),
});

type FormValues = z.infer<typeof formSchema>;

export function PartnerForm() {
  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = React.useState(false);

  const form = useForm<FormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(formSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      businessName: "",
      activityType: undefined,
      location: "",
      referentName: "",
      referentPhone: "",
      referentEmail: "",
      capacity: "",
      hasPower: undefined,
      b2bNotes: "",
    },
    mode: "onTouched",
  });

  const {
    handleSubmit,
    control,
    reset,
    formState: { isSubmitting, errors },
  } = form;

  const onSubmit = async (_values: FormValues) => {
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 800));
    setSubmitted(true);
    toast.success("Candidatura inviata!", {
      description:
        "Grazie per l'interesse. Ti contatteremo per valutare la collaborazione.",
    });
    reset();
  };

  const onError = () => {
    toast.error("Controlla i campi evidenziati", {
      description: "Alcuni campi obbligatori non sono stati compilati.",
    });
  };

  return (
    <section
      id="partner"
      aria-labelledby="partner-title"
      className="relative scroll-mt-24 border-t border-white/5 bg-gradient-to-b from-[#0F0F0F] to-[#101010] py-16 sm:py-20"
    >
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="rounded-3xl border border-white/8 bg-[#1C1C1C]/40 p-6 sm:p-8">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
            aria-expanded={open}
            aria-controls="partner-form-panel"
            className="flex w-full items-start justify-between gap-4 text-left"
          >
            <span className="flex-1">
              <span className="block text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.32em] text-[#D4AF37]">
                Diventa Partner
              </span>
              <span
                id="partner-title"
                className="mt-2 block font-serif text-2xl font-semibold leading-tight text-white sm:text-3xl"
              >
                Sei un operatore del territorio? Diventa Partner Gold Events Sicily Experience.
              </span>
              <span className="mt-2 block max-w-3xl text-sm text-[#A1A1A1] sm:text-base">
                Cantine, agriturismi, ristoranti, guide vulcanologiche, beach
                club: entra nel circuito Gold Events Sicily Experience.
              </span>
            </span>
            <span
              className={cn(
                "mt-1 inline-flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/40 text-[#D4AF37] transition-transform",
                open && "rotate-180",
              )}
              aria-hidden
            >
              <ChevronDown className="size-5" />
            </span>
          </button>

          <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
            {open && (
              <motion.div
                id="partner-form-panel"
                key="panel"
                initial={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
                animate={{ height: "auto", opacity: 1 }}
                exit={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.35, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
                className="overflow-hidden"
              >
                <div className="mt-8 border-t border-white/8 pt-8">
                  {submitted ? (
                    <PartnerSuccess
                      onReset={() => {
                        setSubmitted(false);
                      }}
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <Form {...form}>
                      <form
                        onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit, onError)}
                        className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2"
                        noValidate
                      >
                        <FormField
                          control={control}
                          name="businessName"
                          render={({ field }) => (
                            <FormItem className="sm:col-span-2">
                              <FormLabel className="text-white">
                                Ragione Sociale / Nome Struttura
                              </FormLabel>
                              <FormControl>
                                <Input
                                  {...field}
                                  placeholder="Es. Tenuta dell'Etna S.r.l."
                                  className={fieldClasses(!!errors.businessName)}
                                />
                              </FormControl>
                              <FormMessage />
                            </FormItem>

                            <FormItem>
                              <FormLabel className="text-white">
                                Telefono del Referente

                                  <label
                                    className={cn(
                                      "flex flex-1 cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border px-4 py-2.5 text-sm transition-colors",
                                      field.value === "si"
                                        ? "border-[#D4AF37]/60 bg-[#D4AF37]/10 text-white"
                                        : "border-white/12 bg-[#0F0F0F]/60 text-[#A1A1A1] hover:text-white",
                                    )}
                                  >
                                    <RadioGroupItem
                                      value="si"
                                      className="text-[#D4AF37]"
                                    />
                                    Sì
                                  </label>
                                  <label
                                    className={cn(
                                      "flex flex-1 cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border px-4 py-2.5 text-sm transition-colors",
                                      field.value === "no"
                                        ? "border-[#D4AF37]/60 bg-[#D4AF37]/10 text-white"
                                        : "border-white/12 bg-[#0F0F0F]/60 text-[#A1A1A1] hover:text-white",
                                    )}
                                  >
                                    <RadioGroupItem
                                      value="no"
                                      className="text-[#D4AF37]"
                                    />
                                    No
                                  </label>
                                </RadioGroup>
                              </FormControl>
                              <FormMessage />
                            </FormItem>
                          )}
                        />

                        <FormField
                          control={control}
                          name="b2bNotes"
                          render={({ field }) => (
                            <FormItem className="sm:col-span-2">
                              <FormLabel className="text-white">
                                Tariffe B2B riservate / Note sull'offerta di
                                degustazione o menu{" "}
                                <span className="text-[#A1A1A1]">
                                  (opzionale)
                                </span>
                              </FormLabel>
                              <FormControl>
                                <Textarea
                                  {...field}
                                  placeholder="Descrivi brevemente l'offerta, le disponibilità stagionali, eventuali pacchetti B2B…"
                                  className={cn(
                                    fieldClasses(!!errors.b2bNotes),
                                    "min-h-24",
                                  )}
                                />
                              </FormControl>
                              <FormDescription className="text-[#A1A1A1]">
                                Queste informazioni restano riservate e vengono
                                usate solo per valutare la collaborazione.
                              </FormDescription>
                              <FormMessage />
                            </FormItem>
                          )}
                        />

                        <div className="sm:col-span-2 mt-2 flex flex-col items-stretch gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-end">
                          <Button
                            type="submit"
                            disabled={isSubmitting}
                            variant="outline"
                            className="h-12 rounded-full border-[#D4AF37]/60 bg-transparent px-7 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#D4AF37] transition-all hover:bg-[#D4AF37] hover:text-[#0F0F0F] disabled:opacity-70"
                          >
                            {isSubmitting ? (
                              <>
                                <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin" />
                                Invio in corso…
                              </>
                            ) : (
                              <>
                                <Send className="size-4" strokeWidth={2} />
                                Invia candidatura
                              </>
                            )}
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      </form>
                    </Form>
                  )}
                </div>
              </motion.div>
            )}
          </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function fieldClasses(hasError: boolean) {
  return cn(
    "rounded-lg border bg-[#0F0F0F]/60 text-white placeholder:text-[#6B6B6B]",
    "transition-colors focus-visible:ring-0",
    hasError
      ? "border-[#E5484D]/70 focus-visible:border-[#E5484D]"
      : "border-white/12 focus-visible:border-[#D4AF37]",
  );
}

function PartnerSuccess({ onReset }: { onReset: () => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4 py-10 text-center">
      <span className="inline-flex size-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] text-[#0F0F0F]">
        <Check className="size-8" strokeWidth={2.5} />
      </span>
      <h3 className="font-serif text-2xl font-semibold text-white sm:text-3xl">
        Candidatura inviata!
      </h3>
      <p className="max-w-md text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#A1A1A1] sm:text-base">
        Grazie per l'interesse a entrare nel circuito Gold Events Sicily
        Experience. Ti contatteremo per valutare insieme la collaborazione.
      </p>
      <Button
        type="button"
        onClick={onReset}
        variant="outline"
        className="mt-2 h-11 rounded-full border-[#D4AF37]/60 text-[#D4AF37] hover:bg-[#D4AF37] hover:text-[#0F0F0F]"
      >
        Invia un'altra candidatura
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: src/components/site/register-sw.tsx
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";

/**
 * Client component that registers the service worker (PWA).
 * Renders nothing visible. Mounted once in layout.tsx (inside LangProvider).
 *
 * The service worker lives at /sw.js (public folder). It does NOT cache /api/* or
 * /admin/* (security) and only handles same-origin GET with stale-while-revalidate.
 */
export function RegisterSW() {
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
    if (!("serviceWorker" in navigator)) return;
    // Only register in production OR when explicitly enabled.
    // In dev, Next.js fast-refresh + SW caching fight each other, so we skip.
    if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production") {
      // Allow opt-in via ?sw=1 for local testing.
      const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
      if (params.get("sw") !== "1") return;
    }
    const onLoad = () => {
      navigator.serviceWorker
        .register("/sw.js", { scope: "/" })
        .catch((err) => {
          // Silently fail — PWA is a progressive enhancement.
           
          console.warn("[sw] registration failed:", err);
        });
    };
    if (document.readyState === "complete") onLoad();
    else window.addEventListener("load", onLoad, { once: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener("load", onLoad);
  }, []);

  return null;
}
```

FILE: src/components/site/reveal.tsx
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
"use client";

import { motion, type Variants } from "framer-motion";
import * as React from "react";

interface RevealProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  delay?: number;
  y?: number;
  className?: string;
  as?: "div" | "section" | "article" | "li" | "span";
  once?: boolean;
  amount?: number;
}

const variants: Variants = {
  hidden: (y: number) => ({ opacity: 0, y }),
  visible: {
    opacity: 1,
    y: 0,
    transition: { duration: 0.6, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] },
  },
};

/**
 * Lightweight scroll-reveal wrapper used across the site.
 * Defaults to a 24px slide-up + fade-in.
 */
export function Reveal({
  children,
  delay = 0,
  y = 24,
  className,
  as = "div",
  once = true,
  amount = 0.3,
}: RevealProps) {
  const MotionTag = motion[as] as typeof motion.div;
  return (
    <MotionTag
      className={className}
      variants={variants}
      custom={y}
      initial="hidden"
      whileInView="visible"
      viewport={{ once, amount }}
      transition={{ delay }}
    >
      {children}
    </MotionTag>
  );
}

/**
 * Stagger container — wraps a list of <Reveal> or motion children
 * so they animate in sequence.
 */
export function StaggerGroup({
  children,
  className,
  stagger = 0.08,
  as = "div",
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  stagger?: number;
  as?: "div" | "ul" | "section";
}) {
  const MotionTag = motion[as] as typeof motion.div;
  return (
    <MotionTag
      className={className}
      initial="hidden"
      whileInView="visible"
      viewport={{ once: true, amount: 0.2 }}
      variants={{
        hidden: {},
        visible: { transition: { staggerChildren: stagger } },
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </MotionTag>
  );
}

export const itemVariants: Variants = {
  hidden: { opacity: 0, y: 24 },
  visible: {
    opacity: 1,
    y: 0,
    transition: { duration: 0.55, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] },
  },
};
```

```typescript
import * as React from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Reveal } from "@/components/site/reveal";

interface SectionHeadingProps {
  eyebrow?: string;
  title: React.ReactNode;
  intro?: React.ReactNode;
  align?: "left" | "center";
  className?: string;
  titleClassName?: string;
}

export function SectionHeading({
  eyebrow,
  title,
  intro,
  align = "center",
  className,
  titleClassName,
}: SectionHeadingProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-col gap-4",
        align === "center" ? "items-center text-center" : "items-start text-left",
        className,
      )}
    >
      {eyebrow && (
        <Reveal y={16}>
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.32em] text-[#D4AF37]">
            <span className="h-px w-6 bg-[#D4AF37]/60" aria-hidden />
            {eyebrow}
          </span>
        </Reveal>
      )}
      <Reveal y={20} delay={0.05}>
        <h2
          className={cn(
            "text-balance font-serif text-3xl leading-[1.08] font-semibold text-white sm:text-4xl md:text-[2.75rem]",
            align === "center" && "mx-auto max-w-3xl",
            titleClassName,
          )}
        >
          {title}
        </h2>
      </Reveal>
      {intro && (
        <Reveal y={20} delay={0.1}>
          <p
            className={cn(
              "max-w-2xl text-base leading-relaxed text-[#A1A1A1] sm:text-lg",
              align === "center" && "mx-auto",
            )}
          >
            {intro}
          </p>
        </Reveal>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```typescript
  options: string;

  request: string;
  description: string;

function ServiceCard({

          {service.card}
        </p>

        <Dialog>

            <button
              type="button"
              className="group/cta mt-1 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors hover:text-[#E5C75A]"
            >
              {labels.discover}
              <Plus className="size-3.5 transition-transform group-hover/cta:rotate-90" strokeWidth={2} />
            </button>
          </DialogTrigger>
          <DialogContent className="gold-scroll max-h-[85vh] overflow-y-auto border border-[#D4AF37]/30 bg-[#0F0F0F] p-6 sm:p-8 sm:max-w-xl">
            <DialogHeader className="text-left">
              <div className="mb-3 inline-flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/40 bg-[#D4AF37]/10 text-[#D4AF37]">
                <Icon name={service.icon} className="size-6" />
              </div>

                {service.title}
              </DialogTitle>
              <DialogDescription className="text-[#A1A1A1]">
                {labels.description}
              </DialogDescription>

                />
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-[#0F0F0F] via-[#0F0F0F]/30 to-transparent" />
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-5">
              <DialogBlock label={labels.focus} body={service.focus} />
              <DialogBlock label={labels.offers} body={service.offers} />
              {service.pairing && (
                <DialogBlock
                  label={labels.pairing}
                  body={service.pairing}
                  highlight
                />
              )}
              {service.options && service.options.length > 0 && (
                <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/20 bg-[#1C1C1C]/60 p-4">
                  <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.28em] text-[#D4AF37]">

                    {service.options.map((opt, idx) => (

                        <dt className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-white/55">
                          {opt.label}
                        </dt>
                        <dd className="mt-0.5 text-sm leading-snug text-white/90">

              {/* Direct contact CTAs — WhatsApp + Email + Form */}
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:gap-2.5">
                <a
                  href={`https://wa.me/393442322176?text=${encodeURIComponent(
                    `Ciao Salvo! Sono interessato al servizio "${service.title}". Vorrei ricevere informazioni e un preventivo.`
                  )}`}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="inline-flex h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-[#25D366] px-5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-white transition-colors hover:bg-[#1FB855]"
                >
                  <svg className="size-4" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M17.472 14.382c-.297-.149-1.758-.867-2.03-.967-.273-.099-.471-.148-.67.15-.197.297-.767.966-.94 1.164-.173.199-.347.223-.644.075-.297-.15-1.255-.463-2.39-1.475-.883-.788-1.48-1.761-1.653-2.059-.173-.297-.018-.458.13-.606.134-.133.298-.347.446-.52.149-.174.198-.298.298-.497.099-.198.05-.371-.025-.52-.075-.149-.669-1.612-.916-2.207-.242-.579-.487-.5-.669-.51-.173-.008-.371-.01-.57-.01-.198 0-.52.074-.792.372-.272.297-1.04 1.016-1.04 2.479 0 1.462 1.065 2.875 1.213 3.074.149.198 2.096 3.2 5.077 4.487.709.306 1.262.489 1.694.625.712.227 1.36.195 1.871.118.571-.085 1.758-.719 2.006-1.413.248-.694.248-1.289.173-1.413-.074-.124-.272-.198-.57-.347m-5.421 7.403h-.004a9.87 9.87 0 01-5.031-1.378l-.361-.214-3.741.982.998-3.648-.235-.374a9.86 9.86 0 01-1.51-5.26c.001-5.45 4.436-9.884 9.888-9.884 2.64 0 5.122 1.03 6.988 2.898a9.825 9.825 0 012.893 6.994c-.003 5.45-4.437 9.884-9.885 9.884m8.413-18.297A11.815 11.815 0 0012.05 0C5.495 0 .16 5.335.157 11.892c0 2.096.547 4.142 1.588 5.945L.057 24l6.305-1.654a11.882 11.882 0 005.683 1.448h.005c6.554 0 11.89-5.335 11.893-11.893a11.821 11.821 0 00-3.48-8.413Z"/></svg>
                  WhatsApp
                </a>
                <a
                  href={`mailto:goldeventssicilyexperience@outlook.com?subject=${encodeURIComponent(
                    `Richiesta info: ${service.title}`
                  )}&body=${encodeURIComponent(
                    `Ciao Salvo,\n\nSono interessato al servizio "${service.title}".\nVorrei ricevere un preventivo.\n\nNome:\nData evento:\nLocation:\nNumero ospiti:\n\nGrazie!`
                  )}`}
                  className="inline-flex h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37]/60 px-5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors hover:bg-[#D4AF37] hover:text-[#0F0F0F]"
                >
                  <svg className="size-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M3 8l7.89 5.26a2 2 0 002.22 0L21 8M5 19h14a2 2 0 002-2V7a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v10a2 2 0 002 2z"/></svg>
                  Email
                </a>
              </div>
              <DialogClose asChild>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="inline-flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-white/15 px-6 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#A1A1A1] transition-colors hover:text-white"
                >
                  {labels.close}
                </button>
              </DialogClose>
            </div>
          </DialogContent>
        </Dialog>
      </article>
    </motion.li>
  );
}

function DialogBlock({
  label,
  body,
  highlight = false,
}: {
  label: string;
  body: string;
  highlight?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className={
        highlight
          ? "rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/30 bg-[#D4AF37]/5 p-4"
          : "rounded-xl border border-white/8 bg-[#1C1C1C]/60 p-4"
      }
    >
      <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.28em] text-[#D4AF37]">
        {label}
      </p>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-relaxed text-white/90 sm:text-base">
        {body}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```typescript
                      : "Request this experience"}

        {selected !== null && (
          <ExperienceModal
            exp={sicilyExperiences[selected]}

function ExperienceCard({
  exp,
  lang,
  discoverLabel,
  onOpen,
}: {
  exp: SicilyExperience;
  lang: Lang;
  discoverLabel: string;
  onOpen: () => void;
}) {
  const data = exp[lang];
  return (
    <motion.article
      variants={itemVariants}
      className="group relative flex h-full flex-col gap-3 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-white/8 bg-[#1C1C1C]/70 transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-[#D4AF37]/40 hover:shadow-[0_18px_50px_-20px_rgba(212,175,55,0.4)]"
    >
      {/* Experience image */}
      {exp.image && (
        <div className="relative h-32 w-full overflow-hidden sm:h-36">
          { }
          <img
            src={exp.image}
            alt={data.title}
            loading="lazy"
            className="h-full w-full object-cover opacity-80 transition-all duration-500 group-hover:scale-105 group-hover:opacity-100"
          />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-[#1C1C1C] via-[#1C1C1C]/50 to-transparent" />
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-3 p-5 sm:p-6">
        <span
          aria-hidden
          className="pointer-events-none absolute -top-12 -right-12 size-28 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37]/5 blur-2xl transition-opacity duration-300 group-hover:bg-[#D4AF37]/15"
        />

        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
          <span className="inline-flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/40 bg-[#D4AF37]/10 text-xl text-[#D4AF37] transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105">
            <span aria-hidden>{exp.emoji}</span>
          </span>
          <span className="inline-flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-white/10 text-[#D4AF37]/70">
            <Icon name={exp.icon} className="size-4" />
          </span>
        </div>

        <h3 className="font-serif text-base font-semibold leading-snug text-white sm:text-lg">
          {data.title}
        </h3>
        <p className="flex-1 text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-[#A1A1A1] sm:text-sm">
          {data.description}
        </p>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onOpen}
          className="group/cta mt-1 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors hover:text-[#E5C75A]"
        >
          {discoverLabel}
          <ArrowRight className="size-3.5 transition-transform group-hover/cta:translate-x-1" strokeWidth={2} />
        </button>
      </div>
    </motion.article>
  );
}

function ExperienceModal({
  exp,
  lang,
  discoverLabel: _discoverLabel,
  onClose,
}: {
  exp: SicilyExperience;
  lang: Lang;
  discoverLabel: string;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const data = exp[lang];
  // Close on Escape
  React.useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    // lock scroll
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
      document.body.style.overflow = "";
    };
  }, [onClose]);

  return (
    <motion.div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] flex items-end justify-center sm:items-center"
      initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
      exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
    >
      <motion.div
        className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm"
        onClick={onClose}
        initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
        exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
      />
      <motion.div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label={data.title}
        className="gold-scroll relative z-10 max-h-[85vh] w-full max-w-lg overflow-y-auto rounded-t-3xl border border-[#D4AF37]/30 bg-[#0F0F0F] p-6 sm:rounded-2xl sm:p-8"
        initial={{ y: 40, opacity: 0 }}
        animate={{ y: 0, opacity: 1 }}
        exit={{ y: 20, opacity: 0 }}
        transition={{ duration: 0.3, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="Chiudi"
          className="absolute right-4 top-4 inline-flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-white/10 text-white/70 transition-colors hover:border-[#D4AF37]/50 hover:text-[#D4AF37]"
        >
          <X className="size-4" />
        </button>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span className="inline-flex size-14 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/40 bg-[#D4AF37]/10 text-2xl">
            <span aria-hidden>{exp.emoji}</span>
          </span>
          <span className="inline-flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-white/10 text-[#D4AF37]">
            <Icon name={exp.icon} className="size-4" />
          </span>
        </div>

        <h3 className="mt-5 font-serif text-2xl font-semibold leading-tight text-white sm:text-3xl">
          {data.title}
        </h3>
        <p className="mt-4 text-sm leading-relaxed text-white/85 sm:text-base">
          {data.description}
        </p>

        <div className="mt-7 flex flex-col gap-3 border-t border-white/8 pt-5 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              onClose();
              document
                .querySelector("#contatti")
                ?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
            }}
            className="inline-flex h-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37] px-6 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#0F0F0F] transition-colors hover:bg-[#E5C75A]"
          >
            {lang === "it" ? "Richiedi questa esperienza" : "Request this experience"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </motion.div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}
```

```typescript
"use client";

import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { MapPin, ArrowRight, Plane } from "lucide-react";

function scrollTo(href: string) {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  document.querySelector(href)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
}

/**
 * Hero specific to /sicily-experience — international destination brand identity.
 * Different from the cinematic bivio hero (which lives on /).
 */
export function SicilyHero() {
  return (
    <section
      id="top"
```
