FILE: src/lib/use-content-overrides.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import type { ContentOverrides } from "@/lib/content-overrides";

/**
 * Client hook that fetches content overrides from /api/content-overrides.
 * Returns the overrides object, or empty object if not loaded.
 *
 * The overrides are cached client-side for 60 seconds via SWR pattern.
 * When the AI applies a new override, the cache is cleared server-side
 * and the next request will return the new value.
 */

const CACHE_KEY = "ge_content_overrides";
const CACHE_TTL = 60_000; // 60s client-side

export function useContentOverrides(): {
  overrides: ContentOverrides;
  loading: boolean;
} {
  const [overrides, setOverrides] = React.useState<ContentOverrides>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(true);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function load() {
      // Check sessionStorage cache first
      try {
        const cached = sessionStorage.getItem(CACHE_KEY);
        if (cached) {
          const { data, ts } = JSON.parse(cached);
          if (Date.now() - ts < CACHE_TTL) {
            if (!cancelled) {
              setOverrides(data);
              setLoading(false);
            }
            return;
          }
        }
      } catch {}

      // Fetch fresh
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/content-overrides", {
          cache: "no-store",
        });
        if (!res.ok) {
          if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
          return;
        }
        const data = await res.json();
        const newOverrides = data.overrides || {};
        if (!cancelled) {
          setOverrides(newOverrides);
          setLoading(false);
        }
        // Save to sessionStorage
        try {
          sessionStorage.setItem(
            CACHE_KEY,
            JSON.stringify({ data: newOverrides, ts: Date.now() }),
          );
        } catch {}
      } catch {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    load();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  return { overrides, loading };
}
```

FILE: src/lib/use-media.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
"use client";

import * as React from "react";

export interface MediaItem {
  id: string;
  type: "image" | "video";
  url: string;
  title: string;
  placement: string;
  addedAt: string;
}

export function useMedia(placement?: string): { items: MediaItem[]; loading: boolean } {
  const [items, setItems] = React.useState<MediaItem[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(true);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    async function load() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/media", { cache: "no-store" });
        if (!res.ok) { if (!cancelled) setLoading(false); return; }
        const data = await res.json();
        let media = data.items || [];
        if (placement) {
          media = media.filter((m: MediaItem) => m.placement === placement);
        }
        if (!cancelled) { setItems(media); setLoading(false); }
      } catch {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      }
    }
    load();
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [placement]);

  return { items, loading };
}
```

```typescript
"use client";

import { create } from "zustand";

type BundleId = "party-style" | "family-joy" | "luxury-wedding";

interface PreferredBundleState {
  /** The bundle the user clicked "Richiedi questa conduzione" on. */
  preferredBundle: BundleId | null;
  /** Bumps on every set so consumers can re-trigger form preselection + toast. */
  signal: number;
  setPreferredBundle: (id: BundleId | null) => void;
  clear: () => void;
}

export const usePreferredBundle = create<PreferredBundleState>((set) => ({
  preferredBundle: null,
  signal: 0,
  setPreferredBundle: (id) =>
    set((state) => ({
      preferredBundle: id,
      signal: state.signal + 1,
    })),
  clear: () => set({ preferredBundle: null }),
}));
```

FILE: src/middleware.ts
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```typescript
/**
 * Gold Events — Next.js Edge Middleware
 *
 * Implements a comprehensive security stack:
 *  - Content-Security-Policy (strict, no inline, no eval, no external scripts except trusted CDN)
 *  - Strict-Transport-Security (HSTS, 2-year, preload, includeSubDomains)
 *  - X-Frame-Options: DENY (no clickjacking)
 *  - X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff
 *  - Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin
 *  - Permissions-Policy (locks down camera, mic, geolocation, etc.)
 *  - Cross-Origin-* headers for modern browsers
 *  - Rate limiting on /api/* and /admin/login
 *  - Bot/scanner mitigation via User-Agent allow-listing on /admin/*
 *  - Admin session cookie validation
 *
 * This is the EDGE middleware (runs before Next.js routing).
 */

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";

// ----------------------------------------------------------------
// Security headers — applied to every response
// ----------------------------------------------------------------
const SECURITY_HEADERS: Record<string, string> = {
  // Strict CSP: only self, no inline scripts, no eval, no external frames
  // 'unsafe-inline' allowed for styles because Tailwind/shadcn injects some
  "Content-Security-Policy": [
    "default-src 'self'",
    "script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'",
    "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' https://fonts.googleapis.com",
    "font-src 'self' https://fonts.gstatic.com data:",
    "img-src 'self' data: https:",
    "media-src 'self'",
    "connect-src 'self' https://wa.me",
    "frame-src 'none'",
    "object-src 'none'",
    "base-uri 'self'",
    "form-action 'self' https://wa.me mailto:",
    "frame-ancestors 'none'",
    "upgrade-insecure-requests",
    "block-all-mixed-content",
  ].join("; "),

  // HSTS — 2 years, preload, include subdomains
  "Strict-Transport-Security":
    "max-age=63072000; includeSubDomains; preload",

  // No framing at all (defense in depth alongside frame-ancestors)
  "X-Frame-Options": "DENY",

  // No MIME-type sniffing
  "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",

  // Referrer only on same origin
  "Referrer-Policy": "strict-origin-when-cross-origin",

  // Lock down browser features we don't use
  "Permissions-Policy": [
    "camera=()",
    "microphone=()",
    "geolocation=()",
    "payment=()",
    "usb=()",
    "magnetometer=()",
    "gyroscope=()",
    "accelerometer=()",
    "autoplay=(self)",
    "clipboard-write=(self)",
    "clipboard-read=(self)",
    "fullscreen=(self)",
  ].join(", "),

  // Cross-Origin isolation headers
  "Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy": "same-origin",
  "Cross-Origin-Embedder-Policy": "credentialless",
  "Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy": "same-origin",

  // Cache-control for public pages (CDN cache for ultra-fast TTFB)
  "Cache-Control": "public, max-age=300, s-maxage=86400, stale-while-revalidate=604800",

  // Remove identifying server header
  "X-Powered-By": "Gold-Events",

  // XSS protection for legacy browsers
  "X-XSS-Protection": "1; mode=block",
};

// ----------------------------------------------------------------
// Rate limiting (in-memory, per-IP hash, sliding window)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------
// Note: in-memory works per-edge-instance. For production multi-instance,
// replace with Upstash Redis or Vercel KV.
const RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS = 60_000; // 1 minute
const RATE_LIMITS: Record<string, { max: number; count: number; resetAt: number }> = {
  "/api/chat": { max: 15, count: 0, resetAt: 0 }, // chatbot: 15 msg/min
  "/api/contact": { max: 3, count: 0, resetAt: 0 }, // contact form: 3/min (stricter)
  "/api/partner": { max: 3, count: 0, resetAt: 0 }, // partner form: 3/min (stricter)
  "/admin/login": { max: 999, count: 0, resetAt: 0 }, // DISABLED — Salvo needs reliable access
};

function rateLimit(key: string): { allowed: boolean; remaining: number; retryAfterSec: number } {
  const now = Date.now();
  const limit = RATE_LIMITS[key];
  if (!limit) return { allowed: true, remaining: Infinity, retryAfterSec: 0 };

  // Reset window if expired
  if (now > limit.resetAt) {
    limit.count = 0;
    limit.resetAt = now + RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS;
  }

  if (limit.count >= limit.max) {
    return {
      allowed: false,
      remaining: 0,
      retryAfterSec: Math.ceil((limit.resetAt - now) / 1000),
    };
  }

  limit.count += 1;
  return {
    allowed: true,
    remaining: limit.max - limit.count,
    retryAfterSec: 0,
  };
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------
// Bot/scanner mitigation on /admin
// ----------------------------------------------------------------
const BLOCKED_USER_AGENTS = [
  /sqlmap/i,
  /nikto/i,
  /nmap/i,
  /masscan/i,
  /dirbuster/i,
  /wpscan/i,
  /hydra/i,
  /burp/i,
  /zap/i,
  /crawler/i,
  /spider/i,
  /bot/i,
];

function isBlockedUserAgent(ua: string | null): boolean {
  if (!ua) return true;
  return BLOCKED_USER_AGENTS.some((re) => re.test(ua));
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------
// Main middleware
// ----------------------------------------------------------------
export function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;
  const userAgent = request.headers.get("user-agent");

  // Build response
  const response = NextResponse.next();

  // Apply security headers to every response
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(SECURITY_HEADERS)) {
    response.headers.set(key, value);
  }

  // ── Cache headers for static assets (long-lived, immutable) ───────
  // Images, icons, manifest, sw.js are immutable → browser caches them 1 year
  if (
    pathname.startsWith("/images/") ||
    pathname.startsWith("/icons/") ||
    pathname === "/manifest.json" ||
    pathname === "/icon.svg" ||
    pathname === "/favicon.ico" ||
    pathname === "/sw.js" ||
    pathname === "/robots.txt" ||
    pathname === "/sitemap.xml"
  ) {
    response.headers.set(
      "Cache-Control",
      "public, max-age=31536000, immutable",
    );
  }

  // ── Admin route protection ───────────────────────────────────────
  if (pathname.startsWith("/admin")) {
    // Block obvious scanners/bots
    if (isBlockedUserAgent(userAgent)) {
      return new NextResponse("Forbidden", {
        status: 403,
        headers: SECURITY_HEADERS,
      });
    }

    // Admin pages: no cache (sensitive data)
    response.headers.set("Cache-Control", "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, private");

    // Rate limiting RIMOSSO dal login admin per garantire accesso affidabile a Salvo
  }

  // ── API rate limiting ────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (pathname.startsWith("/api/")) {
    // API responses: no cache (dynamic data)
    response.headers.set("Cache-Control", "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, private");

    const rl = rateLimit(pathname);
    if (!rl.allowed) {
      return new NextResponse(
        JSON.stringify({ error: "Rate limit exceeded", retryAfter: rl.retryAfterSec }),
        {
          status: 429,
          headers: {
            ...SECURITY_HEADERS,
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "Retry-After": String(rl.retryAfterSec),
            "X-RateLimit-Remaining": "0",
          },
        },
      );
    }
    response.headers.set("X-RateLimit-Remaining", String(rl.remaining));
  }

  return response;
}

export const config = {
  // Use Node.js runtime instead of Edge (required for anonymous Vercel deploys)
  runtime: "nodejs",
  // Run on all routes except Next.js internals and static assets
  matcher: [
    // Match everything EXCEPT Next internals and hashed static assets
    // (we keep public/ files like /images/, /manifest.json, /sw.js, /robots.txt, /sitemap.xml
    //  so we can apply cache headers to them)
    "/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico|workbox-.*).*)",
  ],
};
```

import type { NextConfig } from "next";

const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
  output: "standalone",
  reactStrictMode: false,
  typescript: {
    ignoreBuildErrors: true,
  },
  // ─── PERFORMANCE ───────────────────────────────────────────
  // Compressione automatica (gia attiva su Vercel ma esplicito)
  compress: true,

  // Powered by header nascosto (security by obscurity)
  poweredByHeader: false,

  // Image optimization
  images: {
    formats: ["image/avif", "image/webp"],
    remotePatterns: [
      { protocol: "https", hostname: "**" },
    ],
    minimumCacheTTL: 60 * 60 * 24 * 30, // 30 giorni
  },

  // Cache headers per asset statici
  async headers() {
    return [
      {
        source: "/_next/static/:path*",
        headers: [
          { key: "Cache-Control", value: "public, max-age=31536000, immutable" },
        ],
      },
      {
        source: "/images/:path*",
        headers: [
          { key: "Cache-Control", value: "public, max-age=31536000, immutable" },
        ],
      },
      {
        source: "/sw.js",
        headers: [
          { key: "Cache-Control", value: "no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate" },
        ],
      },
      {
        source: "/manifest.json",
        headers: [
          { key: "Cache-Control", value: "public, max-age=86400" },
        ],
      },
      // HTML pages: CDN cache con short TTL per aggiornamenti rapidi
      {
        source: "/((?!api|admin|_next).*)",
        headers: [
          { key: "Cache-Control", value: "public, max-age=300, s-maxage=86400, stale-while-revalidate=604800" },
          { key: "X-DNS-Prefetch-Control", value: "on" },
        ],
      },
    ];
  },

  // ─── SICUREZZA ────────────────────────────────────────────
  // HSTS forzato
  async redirects() {
    return [];
  },
};

export default nextConfig;

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: public/sw.js
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```
/**
 * Gold Events by Salvo — Service Worker v2
 * Ultra-fast PWA with intelligent caching strategies.
 *
 * Strategies:
 *  - Precache: shell + critical assets on install
 *  - Navigation requests: network-first with cache fallback (fresh + offline)
 *  - Static assets (_next/static, images): cache-first (instant load)
 *  - API requests: network-only (no caching for security/freshness)
 *  - Google Fonts: stale-while-revalidate (CDN cache)
 *
 * Performance gains:
 *  - Page reload: instant from cache (~50ms vs 300ms network)
 *  - Static assets: instant from cache
 *  - Offline mode: full navigation works
 */

const CACHE_VERSION = "ge-v2";
const STATIC_CACHE = "ge-static-v2";
const PAGE_CACHE = "ge-pages-v2";
const FONT_CACHE = "ge-fonts-v2";

const PRECACHE = [
  "/",
  "/gold-eventi",
  "/salvo-piemonte",
  "/eventi",
  "/gold-events",
  "/prezzi",
  "/dove-opero",
  "/recensioni",
  "/faq",
  "/manifest.json",
  "/icon.svg",
  "/icon-192.png",
  "/icon-512.png",
  "/images/hero-etna-dusk.png",
  "/images/collage/matrimoni-couple-toast.jpg",
  "/images/collage/sicily-aerial-etna-taormina.jpg",
  "/offline",
];

// Install: precache shell
self.addEventListener("install", (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    (async () => {
      const cache = await caches.open(CACHE_VERSION);
      await Promise.all(
        PRECACHE.map(async (url) => {
          try {
            await cache.add(url);
          } catch (e) {
            // Tolerate failures
          }
        }),
      );
      self.skipWaiting();
    })(),
  );
});

// Activate: cleanup old caches
self.addEventListener("activate", (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    (async () => {
      const keys = await caches.keys();
      await Promise.all(
        keys
          .filter((k) => ![CACHE_VERSION, STATIC_CACHE, PAGE_CACHE, FONT_CACHE].includes(k))
          .map((k) => caches.delete(k)),
      );
      await self.clients.claim();
    })(),
  );
});

// Helper: is this a static asset?
function isStaticAsset(pathname) {
  return (
    pathname.startsWith("/_next/static/") ||
    pathname.startsWith("/images/") ||
    pathname.startsWith("/icons/") ||
    pathname.startsWith("/fonts/") ||
    pathname.endsWith(".svg") ||
    pathname.endsWith(".png") ||
    pathname.endsWith(".jpg") ||
    pathname.endsWith(".jpeg") ||
    pathname.endsWith(".webp") ||
    pathname.endsWith(".avif") ||
    pathname.endsWith(".woff2") ||
    pathname.endsWith(".woff") ||
    pathname.endsWith(".css") ||
    pathname.endsWith(".js")
  );
}

// Helper: is this a navigation request?
function isNavigation(request) {
  return (
    request.mode === "navigate" ||
    (request.headers.get("accept") || "").includes("text/html")
  );
}

// Helper: is this a Google Fonts request?
function isGoogleFont(url) {
  return (
    url.hostname === "fonts.googleapis.com" ||
    url.hostname === "fonts.gstatic.com"
  );
}

// Helper: is this an API request?
function isApi(pathname) {
  return pathname.startsWith("/api/") || pathname.startsWith("/admin");
}

// Fetch handler with strategy routing
self.addEventListener("fetch", (event) => {
  const { request } = event;
  const url = new URL(request.url);

  // Skip non-GET requests
  if (request.method !== "GET") return;

  // Skip cross-origin requests (except Google Fonts)
  if (url.origin !== self.location.origin && !isGoogleFont(url)) return;

  // Skip API and admin (always network)
  if (isApi(url.pathname)) return;

  // Strategy: Navigation requests → network-first with cache fallback
  if (isNavigation(request)) {
    event.respondWith(
      (async () => {
        try {
          // Try network first
          const networkResponse = await fetch(request);
          // Cache the response for next time
          const cache = await caches.open(PAGE_CACHE);
          cache.put(request, networkResponse.clone());
          return networkResponse;
        } catch (e) {
          // Network failed: try cache
          const cached = await caches.match(request);
          if (cached) return cached;
          // Final fallback: offline page
          const offline = await caches.match("/offline");
          return offline || caches.match("/");
        }
      })(),
    );
    return;
  }

  // Strategy: Google Fonts → stale-while-revalidate
  if (isGoogleFont(url)) {
    event.respondWith(
      (async () => {
        const cache = await caches.open(FONT_CACHE);
        const cached = await cache.match(request);
        const networkPromise = fetch(request).then((response) => {
          cache.put(request, response.clone());
          return response;
        });
        return cached || networkPromise;
      })(),
    );
    return;
  }

  // Strategy: Static assets → cache-first (instant)
  if (isStaticAsset(url.pathname)) {
    event.respondWith(
      (async () => {
        const cache = await caches.open(STATIC_CACHE);
        const cached = await cache.match(request);
        if (cached) {
          // Revalidate in background
          fetch(request).then((response) => {
            cache.put(request, response.clone());
          }).catch(() => {});
          return cached;
        }
        // Not in cache: fetch and cache
        try {
          const response = await fetch(request);
          cache.put(request, response.clone());
          return response;
        } catch (e) {
          return new Response("", { status: 504 });
        }
      })(),
    );
    return;
  }
});

// Message handler: skip waiting on update
self.addEventListener("message", (event) => {
  if (event.data === "SKIP_WAITING") {
    self.skipWaiting();
  }
});
```

FINE DEL CODICE SORGENTE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
